hi-score name entry (M2-15 — [front-end-and-attract.md](front-end-and-attract.md#the-name-entry)); the rebind widget and
the UI string table (M2-16 — [options-rebinding-and-accessibility.md](options-rebinding-and-accessibility.md)). Guides: [scenes-and-ui.md](scenes-and-ui.md),
[direct-mode.md](direct-mode.md#the-hud-coreui), [coop.md](coop.md#the-hud-coreui).

| Export | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `createListMenu(items, { focus?, disabledMask?, wrap? })` | function | → `ListMenu` (load time); `items` = 1–31 `MenuItemSpec`s (a label = an action, `{ label, slider }`, `{ label, toggle }`, `{ label, choice }` — M1-17; the first one given wins); focus = the first enabled item at or after `focus` (default 0); `wrap` default `true`; throws `RangeError` for 0 or > 31 items |
| `ListMenu` | class | `items` (frozen `MenuItem`s), `focus`, `disabledMask` (bit `i` = item `i` disabled), `wrap`, `lockTicks` (activation held back after opening), `confirmBuffer`, `repeat` (`DirectionRepeat`), `revision` (bumped on every visible change); `enabled(i)`, `setDisabled(i, disabled)` (a disabled focused item passes the focus on), `focusFirstEnabled(from)`, `open(lockTicks = 0)` (clears the repeat and the buffer) |
| `MenuItem`, `MenuItemKind`, `MenuItemSpec`, `ListMenuOptions` | interface, const + type, type, interface | `{ label, kind, slider, toggle, choice }`; `Action 0`, `Slider 1`, `Toggle 2`, `Choice 3` (M1-17); the `createListMenu` input |
| `createSlider(min, max, step, value)`, `Slider` | function, class | `value` clamped to `min…max`, changed by `step` per Left / Right (clamped); throws `RangeError` when `max < min` or `step ≤ 0` |
| `createToggle(value)`, `Toggle` | function, class | `value: boolean`; Left = off, Right = on, Confirm flips |
| `createChoice(labels, index = 0)`, `Choice` | function, class | One of several labels (M1-17 — the Options screen's CONTROLS): `labels` (1–255, copied and frozen), `index` (clamped into range), `label` (getter); Left / Right step it and wrap, Confirm steps forward; a single label never changes; throws `RangeError` for 0 or > 255 labels |
| `menuTick(menu, input)` | function | → `MenuResult`: Confirm refills the 4-tick buffer; Back wins (`Back`, buffer cleared); while `lockTicks > 0` activation waits (lock and buffer count down, the focus still moves); otherwise a buffered Confirm activates (`Denied` on a disabled item, `Changed` for a toggle or a choice of two or more labels — stepped forward, else `Confirmed` — read `menu.focus`); then the auto-repeated direction: Up / Down move over enabled items (`Moved`), Left / Right change the focused slider / toggle / choice (`Changed`). Never allocates |
| `createConfirm(question)`, `Confirm` | function, class | A YES / NO prompt focused on **NO**: `question`, `focus` (`ConfirmChoice`), `lockTicks`, `confirmBuffer`, `repeat`, `revision`, `open(question?, lockTicks = 0)` (back to NO) |
| `confirmTick(confirm, input)` | function | → `MenuResult`: Left / Up → YES, Right / Down → NO (`Moved`, no wrap), a buffered Confirm → `Confirmed` (read `focus`), Back → `Back`; the same buffer and lock rules as `menuTick`. Never allocates |
| `ConfirmChoice` | const + type | `Yes 0`, `No 1` |
| `MenuResult`, `menuResultSfx(result)` | const + type, function | `None 0`, `Moved 1`, `Changed 2`, `Confirmed 3`, `Back 4`, `Denied 5`; → `SFX_CUES.MenuMove` (moved / changed), `MenuSelect` (confirmed), `MenuBack` (back **and** denied), `-1` for none |
| `repeatDirections(state, input)`, `DirectionRepeat` | function, class | → one direction bit to act on this tick: a new press at once (lowest bit wins), the held one after `MENU_REPEAT_DELAY` ticks and then every `MENU_REPEAT_INTERVAL`; a latched tap acts once; `{ dir, held, reset() }` |
| `MENU_REPEAT_DELAY`, `MENU_REPEAT_INTERVAL`, `MENU_CONFIRM_BUFFER_TICKS` | const | `18`, `6`, `4` |
| `drawPanel(list, x, y, w, h, fill?, border?, alpha = 232)` | function | A filled box with a 1-px border (5 rects) |
| `drawMenu(list, menu, stringBase, layout, text = DEFAULT_UI_TEXT)` | function | → the y below the last row: labels (focus colour + `→` cursor, disabled dimmed), a slider's 50-px bar and value, a toggle's `ON` / `OFF`, a choice's current label; uses string slots `stringBase … + menuStringSlots(menu) − 1`, written only when changed. `layout` must be a constant (a literal per call allocates) |
| `MenuLayout` | interface | `x`, `y`, `lineHeight?` (12), `align?` (`TextAlign`, Left), `cursorX?` (`x − 10`), `valueX?` (`x + 80` — slider bars, `ON` / `OFF`, choice labels) |
| `menuStringSlots(menu)`, `CONFIRM_STRING_SLOTS` | function, const | `items + 3 + choices` (items, `ON`, `OFF`, cursor, then one per choice item for its label — M1-17); `4` (question, YES, NO, cursor) |
| `drawConfirm(list, confirm, stringBase, cx, cy, text = DEFAULT_UI_TEXT)` | function | A 176×52 opaque panel centred at `(cx, cy)` with the question (≤ 2 lines) and YES / NO, the focused one highlighted with the cursor |
| `UI_COLORS` | const | `text`, `focus`, `disabled`, `panel`, `border`, `title`, `alert`, `track` (0xRRGGBB) |
| `buildHud(world, list, sprites?, bossHp = false, text = DEFAULT_UI_TEXT)` | function | Clears `list` and draws the whole HUD (top bar `1P` / `HI` / `2P` or `------` — with `bossHp` (M2-09, the `bossHpBar` display option) and `world.bosses.hpBar.visible`, `BOSS` (red) at x 148 and a 4-px, `BOSS_HP_BAR_WIDTH`-wide frame at x 176 with a 2-px red fill of `bossHpBarFill(bar, 62)` replace `HI` and its number —, stock icons, the 7-slot meter with its flashing highlight and greyed slots — labelled by `meterLabelFrame` since M2-03 —, Force Field pips — in Direct mode (M2-05) the tier pips instead: `SHOT` (one pip per level above 0 of the current family, lit in `HUD_FAMILY_COLORS`), `SUB`, `ARM` (one per hit, in the tier's `HUD_ARM_COLORS`; none without an Arm), `SPD` and the family's `label`); in a co-op game (M2-06) a joinable slot's blinking `PRESS START` on the top bar and, while both ships are active, two 192-px compact halves (stock icon + count, the seven 20-px meter boxes with `METER_SHORT_LABELS` and shield pips — or `SH` / `SB` / `AR` / `SP` pips — and `PRESS START` / `GAME OVER` for an out player); **clears the scores' `displayDirty` and `hiScoreDirty`**; < `HUD_COMMAND_COUNT` commands, string slots 0–3 (meter) / 0–8 (Direct) / 0–21 (co-op) / 22 (`BOSS`, M2-09) — those strings are written only when drawn; never allocates |
| `createHud(sprites?, text = DEFAULT_UI_TEXT)`, `Hud` | function, class | M2-16: the HUD's words from `text` (the scene flow passes its table). `Hud { sprites, builds, showBossHp (M2-09 — draw the boss HP bar; the scene flow sets it from the saved option every displayed frame), update(world, list) → rebuilt?, invalidate() }` — `update` compares the dirty flags and, per player (M2-06 — two typed arrays of 12 values each), its `HudPlayerState`, whether it plays, lives, the meter cursor and equippable mask, the shield's hits / max / tier and (M2-05) `shot` / `sub` / `family` and speed level, plus the flash phase (only while a slot is highlighted) the `PRESS START` blink (only while a prompt shows) and, with `showBossHp`, the boss HP bar's fill in pixels (M2-09), and calls `buildHud` only on a change (or a different World / list) |
| `HUD_LAYOUT`, `HUD_COLORS`, `HUD_STRING_SLOTS`, `HUD_METER_FLASH_TICKS` | const | Positions (`p1X` 8, `hiX` 156, `p2X` 292, numbers `+16`, `digits` 8, `stockX` 4 / 10 px / `stockIcons` 5, `meterX` 58, `slotW` 40, `shieldX` 344, `topY` 0, `bottomY` 208; M2-05 Direct mode: `shotX` 58, `subX` 130, `armX` 196, `speedX` 252, `familyX` 306; M2-06 co-op, relative to a half: `halfW` 192, `coopStockX` 2, `coopMeterX` 22, `coopSlotW` 20, `coopShieldX` 164, `coopShotX` 22, `coopSubX` 68, `coopArmX` 114, `coopSpeedX` 148; M2-09: `bossX` 148, `bossBarX` 176); the bar and label colours (M2-05: `pipOff`, `subPip`, `speedPip`, `pipLabel`; M2-06: `prompt`, `over`, `slotOn`, `slotLit`, `slotOff`; M2-09: `bossLabel`, `bossFrame`, `bossFill`); `{ p1: 0, hi: 1, p2: 2, dashes: 3 }` + M2-05 `shot: 4, sub: 5, arm: 6, speed: 7, family: 8` + M2-06 `pressStart: 9, gameOver: 10, shortShot: 11, shortSub: 12, shortArm: 13, shortSpeed: 14, meterShort: 15` (… 21) + M2-09 `boss: 22`; `8` + M3-01 `time: 23` (the caravan's `TIME`) |
| `HUD_COMMAND_COUNT`, `HUD_STRING_COUNT` | const | `100`, `24` since M3-01 (`23` since M2-09 (`96`, `22` since M2-06; `64`, `9` in M2-05)) — the size a HUD draw list needs (the game scene's `hudList`) |
| `hudClockSeconds(world)` | function | M3-01: → the whole seconds the HUD shows of a caravan clock, `ceil(timeLeft / 60)` (0 at the end); `buildHud` draws `TIME` and them in player 2's place (red for the last ten) whenever `world.timeLeft ≥ 0`, and `Hud.update` rebuilds when they change. Never allocates |
| `BOSS_HP_BAR_WIDTH`, `BossHpBarView`, `bossHpBarFill(bar, width)` | const, interface, function | M2-09: `64` — the bar's frame in the top HUD bar (the fill is 2 px narrower); `{ visible, hp, maxHp }` (`core/bosses` `BossHpBar` satisfies it); → the fill in whole pixels: `⌈hp × width / maxHp⌉`, at most `width`, 0 when hidden, empty or unusable — at least 1 px while any hit point is left, full only at full strength |
| `HudPlayerState`, `hudPlayerState(world, slot)` | const + type, function | M2-06: `Playing 0`, `Join 1` (a co-op slot that may drop in), `Continue 2` (out, may continue), `Out 3` (out for good), `Absent 4` (no ship, no way in); → one player's state (`core/world` `playerCanJoin`'s rule repeated — `core/world` imports `core/ui`), `Absent` for a slot the World lacks; never allocates |
| `METER_SHORT_LABELS`, `METER_SHORT_IDS`, `HUD_PROMPT_BLINK_TICKS` | const | M2-06: two letters per `METER_LABEL_FRAMES` entry for the co-op halves (`SP MS DB LS OP ? ! SB 2W TP TL VT FW RP CY TW` — since M2-16 the English table's texts of `METER_SHORT_IDS`, the UI string ids the HUD draws them from); `32` — the `PRESS START` blink's half period |
| `HUD_FAMILY_COLORS`, `HUD_ARM_COLORS` | const | M2-05: the lit `SHOT` pips per main family (index = `Loadout.family`, wrapping): Beam → Disc orange `0xf87838`, Laser → Wave blue `0x58b8f8`; the `ARM` pips per tier − 1: green, silver, gold |
| `METER_LABEL_FRAMES`, `meterLabelFrame(world, slot)` | const, function | M2-03: the 16 `hud/meter-labels` frames by label (`SPEED` … `!`, then `SPREAD`, `2-WAY`, `TORPEDO`, `TAIL`, `VERTICAL`, `FREE WAY`, `RIPPLE`, `CYCLONE`, `TWIN`); → the frame a slot shows in a World: MISSILE / DOUBLE / LASER the label of the arsenal's weapon in that role (`core/weapons` `WEAPON_BEHAVIOR_LABELS`; the slot's own for an empty role or an unlabelled behaviour), every other slot its own — never allocates. M3-01 appended the seven Extra Edit labels (`CONTROL`, `UPPER`, `SMALL SP`, `HAWK`, `2-WAY BK`, `BACK DBL`, `SPR GUN`) and their compact `meterShort…` ids in `METER_SHORT_IDS` |
| `UI_SPRITES`, `UiSprites`, `resolveUiSprites(content)` | const, interface, function | `hud/life`, `hud/life@p2` (player 2's stock icon, M2-06), `hud/meter-slot` (frames normal / highlighted / disabled), `hud/meter-labels` (the 7 slot labels, then the Types B–D weapon names — 16 frames since M2-03), `ui/logo`, since M2-14 the ending scenes' pieces `ui/ending-citadel`, `ui/ending-ark`, `ui/ending-blast` (4 frames), `ui/ending-bubble`, `ui/ending-sun`, `ui/ending-surface` (tiling, 64 px) — part of `ENGINE_SPRITES`; → `{ life, lifeP2, meterSlot, meterLabels, logo, endingCitadel, endingArk, endingBlast, endingBubble, endingSun, endingSurface }` ids, `-1` for a sprite the content lacks (drawn as rectangles / text instead) |
| `createNameEntry(length = NAME_ENTRY_LENGTH)`, `NameEntry` | function, class | M2-15 (shmup_feat.md §17): the 3-letter name entry — `length`, `glyphs` (`Int8Array`: a `NAME_ENTRY_GLYPHS` index per letter, -1 = empty), `cursor` (a letter, or `length` = `END`), `done`, `lockTicks`, `confirmBuffer`, `repeat`, `revision`, `open(lockTicks = 0)` (`A` then empty letters), `letter(i)` (the glyph's one-character string, `''` while empty — never builds one), `name` (getter: the letters, an empty one as a space, trailing spaces trimmed — builds a string); the first letter starts on `A`; throws `RangeError` for a length outside 1–8 |
| `nameEntryTick(entry, input)` | function | M2-15: → `MenuResult` — a Confirm press refills the 4-tick buffer; Back = Left (buffer cleared); while locked the buffered OK waits; OK on a letter → `Moved` (next), on `END` → `Confirmed` (`done`); the auto-repeated direction: Up / Down step the letter through the glyphs (wrapping; an empty letter starts at `A` up, at the space down — `Changed`), Left / Right move the cursor (`Moved`); a finished entry → `None`. Never allocates |
| `drawNameEntry(list, entry, stringBase, cx, y, blinkOff = false, text = DEFAULT_UI_TEXT)` | function | M2-15: one 16-px cell per letter centred on `cx` (the letter or `_`, an underline), then `END`; the cursor's cell in the focus colour with `↑` / `↓` above and below (hidden while `blinkOff`); string slots `stringBase … + NAME_ENTRY_STRING_SLOTS − 1`, written only when changed; never allocates |
| `NAME_ENTRY_GLYPHS`, `NAME_ENTRY_LENGTH`, `NAME_ENTRY_STRING_SLOTS` | const | M2-15: `'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789.-! '` (Up order); `3`; `NAME_ENTRY_LENGTH + 4` (the letters, `END`, `↑`, `↓`, `_`) |
| `REBINDABLE_ACTIONS` | const | M2-16 (shmup_feat.md §4 "[P1] Rebinding"): per binding context the actions the rebind screen offers, in row order — `game`: `Up, Down, Left, Right, Shot, Sub, PowerUp, Special, Speed, Pause`; `menu`: `Up, Down, Left, Right, Confirm, Back, Pause` |
| `RebindStatus` | const + type | M2-16: a rebinding's outcome (`@shmup/input-web` `rebindAction`) — `Bound 0`, `Moved 1` (the key taken from another action that keeps other keys), `Swapped 2` (the other action had only that key and takes the old ones), `Refused 3` (a required action would be left without a key), `Unchanged 4`, `Rejected 5` (a reserved key, one the device cannot hold, a split keyboard's player-2 key) |
| `CaptureStatus`, `REBIND_CAPTURE_TICKS` | const + type, const | M2-16: `Idle 0`, `Waiting 1`, `Captured 2`, `Cancelled 3` — the rebinding capture's state (`WebInput.capture.status`, `ControlsSetup.pollCapture()`); `300` (5 s — the prompt's timeout) |
| `RebindEvent` | const + type | M2-16: what `rebindTick` reports — `None 0`, `Context 1` (MODE changed), `Capture 2` (OK on an action row), `Reset 3`, `Done 4` (OK on DONE, or Back) |
| `createRebindPanel(labels)`, `RebindPanel`, `RebindPanelLabels` | function, class, interface | M2-16: the rebind widget — `contextChoice` (MODE), `menus` (per context: MODE, the actions' rows, RESET, DONE), `keys` (per context and row, the keys shown — `-` at first), `capturing`, `captureTicks`, `prompt`, `message`, `result` (the last `MenuResult`, for the sound), `revision`, `menuSlots` / `maxRows` (precomputed); getters `context`, `menu`, `focusedAction` (`null` on MODE / RESET / DONE), `resetRow`; `setKeys(context, action, label)`, `say(message)`, `startCapture(prompt)`, `stopCapture()` (re-locks the rows 2 ticks), `open(context, lockTicks)`. Labels: `{ mode, contexts, actions, reset, done }` |
| `rebindTick(panel, input)` | function | M2-16 → `RebindEvent`: while capturing only the prompt's clock runs (the scene reads the host's capture); else `menuTick` on the shown context's rows — Back or OK on DONE → `Done`, a MODE change → `Context` (the other list, focused on MODE), OK on an action → `Capture`, on RESET → `Reset`. Never allocates |
| `drawRebindPanel(list, panel, stringBase, layout, hint, text = DEFAULT_UI_TEXT)`, `rebindStringSlots(panel)` | function, function | M2-16: the rows (`drawMenu`) with each action's keys at `layout.valueX`, the message under them, and while capturing a 240×48 box over the rows with the prompt, `hint` and a bar draining over `REBIND_CAPTURE_TICKS`; writes a slot only when its text changed, never allocates (index loops — M2-16's allocation lesson); → `menuSlots + maxRows + 3` |
| `UiText`, `UiTextId`, `DEFAULT_UI_TEXT`, `UI_TEXT_IDS` | types, const, const | M2-16 (`ui/strings.ts`, shmup_feat.md §21 localization): a resolved UI string table (every id → its text, frozen); a fixed id; the built-in English table (290 ids: the fixed ones plus one `sfx.<CueName>` per `SFX_CUES` cue, the sound test's names) — `content/strings/en.strings.json` holds the same; every id in table order |
| `MAX_UI_TEXT_LENGTH`, `DEFAULT_LANGUAGE` | const | M2-16: `48` (a `strings` entry's longest text); `'en'` (the language the shipped game shows — the choice is M3) |
| `resolveUiText(table)` | function | M2-16 → a frozen table: the given entries over `DEFAULT_UI_TEXT` (a missing, empty or non-string entry keeps English; unknown ids ignored) — `DEFAULT_UI_TEXT` itself for `null` / `undefined` or when nothing differs |
| `formatUiText(template, a, b = '')` | function | M2-16 → the template with `{0}` / `{1}` replaced (`formatUiText('ZONE {0} CLEAR', 'B')`); builds a string — transitions only, never per frame |
| `TextMetrics` | type | Re-exported from `presentation` |

### `scenes` — scene stack and the scene flow (implemented)

The scene stack and the M1 scene set (M1-16), the Options screen and the saved hi-scores in the
flow (M1-17), the difficulty menu and the continue countdown (M2-01), the weapon select with its
live preview and the Auto Power-Up order editor (M2-03), the ship select (M2-05), `1 PLAYER` /
`2 PLAYERS` and the co-op rules (M2-06), campaign runs — the run state, the zone tally, the zone
map, the ending hook, hidden bonus stages, practice (M2-10), the ending scenes and the credits
(M2-14 — [zones-h-and-i.md](zones-h-and-i.md#the-endings)), the front end — the mode select, the
attract loop, the name entry, the hi-score tables, the practice select, the sound test (M2-15 —
[front-end-and-attract.md](front-end-and-attract.md); `scenes` is `implemented` since then), the
Options pages, the rebind screen, the input test, the save's game options in the next games'
configs and the UI string table (M2-16 —
[options-rebinding-and-accessibility.md](options-rebinding-and-accessibility.md)), the EXTRA menu
and its modes (boss rush, caravan, arcade loops), the secret codes, the assists, whole-run replays
recorded by the flow and the replay browser and playback (M3-01 —
[extra-modes-and-replays.md](extra-modes-and-replays.md)), the EXTRAS options page, the DISPLAY
page's CRT and ASPECT rows and the final zone's escape sequence (M3-02 —
[visual-and-mechanic-extras.md](visual-and-mechanic-extras.md)). Guides:
[scenes-and-ui.md](scenes-and-ui.md), [saves-and-options.md](saves-and-options.md), [coop.md](coop.md#the-scene-flow-corescenes-and-gameinputseats),
[campaign-and-bonus-stages.md](campaign-and-bonus-stages.md),
[direct-mode.md](direct-mode.md#the-ship-select-corescenes),
[difficulty-and-rank.md](difficulty-and-rank.md#the-difficulty-menu-difficultyscene),
[meter-arsenal.md](meter-arsenal.md#the-weapon-select-corescenes).

| Export | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `createSceneStack()`, `SceneStack` | function, class | Depth `SCENE_STACK_DEPTH` (8): `depth`, `top`, `pending`, `revision`, `capacity`, `sceneAt(i)` (0 = bottom — not `at()`: the Chrome-69 lint rule rejects any `.at(` call), `contains(scene)`, `push` / `pop` / `replace` / `reset(scene)` (deferred while a scene ticks, applied in request order at the end of the tick — also those the hooks request — at once otherwise), `tick(input)` (the top scene only, then `flush()`), `flush()`. Throws `RangeError` on a full stack, a scene already on it, more than 8 requests in one tick or a runaway chain (> 64); never allocates |
| `Scene` | interface | `id`, `overlay` (the scene below stays drawn, frozen), `inputContext` (`'game'` / `'menu'`), `dim` (0…1 under the UI), `uiRevision` (bumped when `drawUi` would draw something else), `enter()`, `exit()`, `cover()`, `uncover()`, `tick(input)`, `drawUi(list)` |
| `SceneId` | type | `'boot' \| 'title' \| 'game' \| 'pause' \| 'stageClear' \| 'gameOver' \| 'confirm' \| 'options' \| 'difficulty' \| 'continue'` (the last two M2-01) \| `'weaponSelect' \| 'autoOrder'` (M2-03) \| `'shipSelect'` (M2-05) \| `'map'`, `'ending'` (M2-10) \| `'credits'` (M2-14) \| `'nameEntry'`, `'hiScore'`, `'demo'`, `'story'`, `'practice'`, `'soundTest'` (M2-15 — the skeleton's placeholder ids `'attract'` / `'select'` are gone) \| `'controls'`, `'display'`, `'gameOptions'`, `'rebind'`, `'inputTest'` (M2-16) |
| `createSceneFlow(host, start = 'boot')` | function | → `SceneFlow`, started on `'boot'` / `'title'` / `'game'` (`SceneStart`). Creates every scene, menu and draw list and the game scene's placeholder World (then **clears `host.events`**); throws `RangeError` if the scenes need more than 512 UI string slots (M2-16; 384 since M2-15, 256 since M2-14, 224 since M2-10, 192 before, 160 before M2-05, 96 before M2-03 — the UI list has 384 commands since M2-10, 256 before), or what `host.createWorld()` / `withDifficulty` throws. Builds one config per difficulty preset (the host's for its own preset, `withDifficulty` of it with the content's `rules` table — or the built-in one — for the others) and each power-up mode's and preset's session hi-score from the save's tables (M2-01; per mode since M2-05), and the ship select's ships (the content's, `DEFAULT_PLAYER_SHIP` without any — M2-05). `createGame(…, { scenes })` calls it. Since M2-10 it also decides the campaign: `SceneFlow.campaign` is `content.campaign` when `config.stage` is its start zone's stage, else `null` (single-stage runs); since M2-15 it decodes the content's demos once (`SceneFlow.demos`; a demo that does not decode or names a missing stage is left out) and flattens the campaign's story into the `StoryScene`'s rows; since M2-16 it resolves the content's `en` UI string table (`SceneFlow.text`, `buildSceneLabels`), starts on the saved difficulty (`save.options.game.difficulty`, else the host config's) and arms every difficulty's config with the save's sim-affecting options (`withUserGameOptions`) |
| `SceneFlowHost` | interface | `config`, `content`, `events`, `exit` (`platform.exit` or `null` — no EXIT item, Back on the title only backs out), `createWorld(config?)` (a fresh World pushing into `events`; `config` = the chosen difficulty's — omitted = the host's, M2-01), `save?` (a `SaveStore`; omitted / `null` = memory-only with the defaults — M1-17), `inputProfiles?` (`InputProfileSetup`; omitted / `null` = CONTROLS disabled — M1-17), `soundTest?` (`SoundTestSetup`; omitted / `null` or no titles = the sound test's MUSIC row disabled — M2-15), `controls?` (`ControlsSetup`; omitted / `null` = REBIND KEYS / PAD disabled — M2-16), M3-01 `replays?` (`ReplayLibrary`; omitted / `null` = runs recorded but not stored, REPLAYS disabled), `shareReplay?` (`(text) => boolean`; omitted / `null` = no SHARE — the TV), `buildId?` (default `'dev'`) |
| `ControlsSetup` | interface | M2-16 — the rebind screen's host side (`@shmup/shell` `createShellControls` implements it over `@shmup/input-web`): `devices()` → `RebindDevice[]` (the key / remote profile in use, then the gamepad profile), `keysLabel(device, context, action)` (`Z  SPACE`, `-`), `beginCapture(device)`, `pollCapture()` → a `CaptureStatus` (every tick while the prompt is up — must not allocate), `endCapture()`, `bindCaptured(device, context, action)` → `RebindOutcome` (conflict detection; stored in the save's `options.input.bindings` and applied at once), `reset(device, context)` |
| `RebindDevice`, `RebindDeviceKind`, `RebindOutcome` | interfaces, type | M2-16: `{ id, kind }` (a profile id — the rebinding is stored per profile — and what it drives); `'keyboard' \| 'remote' \| 'gamepad'`; `{ status (a RebindStatus), other (the other action involved, or null) }` |
| `SoundTestSetup` | interface | M2-15: `{ music: readonly string[] }` — the music library's track titles in library order; the sound test's MUSIC row offers them and pushes `SimEventKind.SoundTest` with the chosen index (`@shmup/shell` passes `@shmup/audio-web`'s music content titles) |
| `InputProfileSetup` | interface | `{ choices: InputProfileChoice[], active: string \| null }` — the profiles CONTROLS steps through, in order, and the id in use (`null` / unknown → the first choice is shown) (M1-17) |
| `SceneFlow` | interface | `stack`, the scenes (`boot`, `title`, `game`, `pause`, `stageClear`, `gameOver`, `confirm`, `options`, since M2-01 `difficultyMenu`, `continueScreen`, since M2-03 `weaponSelect`, `autoOrder`, since M2-05 `shipSelect`, since M2-10 `map`, `ending`, since M2-14 `credits`, since M2-15 `nameEntry`, `hiScores`, `demo`, `story`, `practiceSelect`, `soundTest`, since M2-16 `controlsPage`, `displayPage`, `gameOptionsPage`, `rebind`, `inputTest`), since M2-16 `text` (the resolved `UiText` every scene draws with) and `labels` (`SceneLabels`), since M2-15 `demos` (the decoded attract demos), since M2-10 `run` (the `RunState`), `campaign` (`CampaignSpec \| null`) and `startPractice(zoneId, checkpoint = -1, loadout = null)` (→ `true` when a practice run of that campaign zone starts at that checkpoint — its rank stage term, a fresh start with the loadout (M2-15; `null` = the config's), its stage prepared, a game start counted, the stack reset to the game; `false` without a campaign, for an unknown zone or a bad checkpoint; since M2-15 its scores go into its `-practice` table with the name entry, its clear returns to the title), `save` (the host's store or a memory-only one), `modeKey` (`hiScoreModeKey(gameConfig, mode)` — the table the next game goes into, one per power-up mode and difficulty; since M2-15 a co-op game's own `-2p` table), `difficulty` (M2-01: the preset of the next game — the host config's until one is chosen under START; since M2-16 the saved choice at start), `gameConfig` (M2-01: the config the next game's World gets — since M2-03 with the weapon select's loadout, `withArsenal`, since M2-05 with the ship select's ship, `withShip`, since M2-16 with the save's sim-affecting options, `withUserGameOptions`), `arsenal` (M2-03: the `ArsenalChoice` of the last START, `{}` before), M2-05: `ship` (the `ShipChoice` of the ship select, `null` before — the host config's ship flies), `ships` (what it offers), `inputProfiles` (the choices, `[]` = CONTROLS disabled), `activeInputProfile` (index of the profile in use, −1 = none of them; changed by the Options screen), `inputContext`, M2-06: `coop` (the title's choice — the next games are co-op ones) and `inputSeats` (`2` while the game scene or the continue countdown is on top with a `config.coop` World, else `1` — `Game.inputSeats` returns it), `world` (the game scene's), `menuInput` (every player merged, reused), `hiScore` (the session's best for the chosen difficulty — and, since M2-05, the chosen ship's power-up mode), `view` (`SceneFlowView`), `tick(input)`, `updateFrame()` (once per displayed frame: World view + HUD while the game is visible — the weapon select's preview view, no HUD, while that screen is visible (M2-03) —, the top dim, the UI list rebuilt only when the visible set or a `uiRevision` changed), `setBootProgress(fraction, label?)`, `finishBoot()`, `onResume()` (pause menu over a running game), `setHiScore(value)` (raises the chosen difficulty's and mode's; floored, capped at `MAX_SCORE`). Each session hi-score starts from the save's best of its table (M1-17; per difficulty since M2-01, per power-up mode and difficulty since M2-05). M3-01: `extra`, `replaysScreen`, `replayScreen` (the new scenes), `recorder` (`RunRecorder` — the run in progress), `lastReplay` (the last finished run's replay, or `null`), `speedPercent` (the game-speed assist while the game scene is on top, else 100 — `core/game` slows its clock by it), `noteWorldEdited()` (the debug tools changed a World outside a tick: the run is not saved), `chooseMode(mode, zone?, loop?)` (the next game start's `RunMode`, the CARAVAN's zone, the ARCADE's first loop); M3-02 `extras` (the EXTRAS page) and `FlowControl.enterEscape()` (the final zone's stage clear takes `ClearNext.Escape` and swaps the World for the escape stage's) |
| `SceneFlowView` | interface | `tick`, `world` (`WorldView \| null` — the game's, or since M2-03 the weapon select's preview, since M2-15 the attract demo's), `hud` (the game scene's HUD — or the demo play's own, M2-15), `ui`, `dim` — what `Game.renderFrame()` copies |
| `SceneStart` | type | `'boot' \| 'title' \| 'game'` |
| `BootScene` | class | `progress`, `label`, `done`; holds until `finishBoot()`, then the title |
| `TitleScene` | class | `menu` — the **mode select** (M2-15; `TitleItem` order): `1 PLAYER` / `2 PLAYERS` (M2-06; `1 PLAYER` was START before) / PRACTICE (M2-15 — the practice select; disabled without a campaign) / OPTIONS (the Options screen, enabled since M1-17) / SOUND TEST (M2-15) / EXIT only with `platform.exit`, `phase` (0 `PRESS OK`, 1 menu), `menuOpen`, `idle` (M2-15: ticks on `PRESS OK` without a press or a held key — at `TITLE_ATTRACT_TICKS` `startAttract()`: the demo play, or the hi-score tables without demos; the open menu never idles out); the logo, a 32-tick blink, the chosen difficulty's session hi-score (from the save's best), the title music; `1 PLAYER` / `2 PLAYERS` choose the player count (`choosePlayers` — every difficulty's config `withCoop`, M2-06) and push the `DifficultyScene` (M2-01 — before, START replaced the title with the game); Back → the exit confirmation, or (no exit) back to `PRESS OK`. M3-01: EXTRA (`TitleItem.Extra` 5 → the `ExtraScene`; EXIT is 6) and the title's secret codes (EXTRA SHIPS, EXTRA EDIT — `SecretCodeTracker`, a jingle and a message) |
| `GameScene` | class | `world`, `hud`, `hudList` (`HUD_COMMAND_COUNT` commands, `HUD_STRING_COUNT` strings — M2-05), `starts`, `restart(retry = true)` (also counts `gamesStarted` in the save — M1-17; since M2-10 the run's current zone from its entry state — `FlowControl.createRunWorld(run.entry)` —, a retry notes the old World for the run and leaves a bonus stage; since M2-16 a retry first takes the options changed since the run began at the run's own difficulty — `FlowControl.rearmRun()`; every World of a run is built from `FlowControl.runConfig`, taken when the run began, so option changes never reach the next zone or a bonus stage; `enter` begins a run or plays the zone the map / practice prepared), `swapWorld(world)` (M2-10: into a bonus stage and back); `inputContext` `'game'`; Pause / Back (any player, read per player since M2-06) → pause menu — except a co-op player's `JOIN_ACTIONS` press while it may join (`playerCanJoin`: the World joins it instead); each start creates the World with `flow.gameConfig` (M2-01); opens stage clear `STAGE_CLEAR_DELAY_TICKS` / game over `GAME_OVER_DELAY_TICKS` World ticks after the status changed — for a game over with continues left (`core/world` `canContinue`) the `ContinueScene` instead (M2-01); draws the boss WARNING band and (M2-10, campaign runs) the zone title card for `ZONE_CARD_TICKS`; an opened bonus entrance warps after `BONUS_WARP_TICKS`, a death in a bonus stage returns after `BONUS_FAIL_TICKS` |
| `PauseScene` | class | Overlay, dim `PAUSE_DIM`: `menu` (RESUME / OPTIONS — the Options screen over the frozen game, M1-17 / RETRY STAGE / QUIT TO TITLE); Pause / Back / RESUME resume, RETRY restarts without a confirmation, QUIT asks |
| `OptionsScene` | class | Overlay, dim `PAUSE_DIM`, an opaque 288×192 panel at y 12 (M1-17; regrouped in M2-16): `master`, `music`, `sfx` (`Slider`s 0–10, step 1), `menu` (`OptionsItem` order — the sliders, CONTROLS, DISPLAY, GAME, BACK); `enter()` reads the save's volumes, focus MASTER, 2-tick lock (again on `uncover`); a slider change pushes `MasterVolume` / `MusicVolume` / `SfxVolume` at once with the move sound; OK on a slider is silent; OK on CONTROLS / DISPLAY / GAME pushes that page; BACK / Back store the levels (`save.setOptions`), `save.flush()`, `MenuBack`, pop. (Until M2-16 the profile and the display rows were rows of this screen) |
| `DisplayScene` | class | M2-16, id `display` — the DISPLAY page (the M2-02 / M2-08 / M2-09 rows, unchanged behaviour): `bullets` (`Choice` of the bullet palettes), `scale`, `shake` (`Toggle`), `flashes`, `hitbox`, `bossHp`, `menu` (`DisplayItem` order); `enter()` reads the save's display options; a change pushes `BulletPalette` / `ScaleMode` (the index) or `ScreenShake` / `ReduceFlashing` / `ShowHitbox` / `BossHpBar` (1 / 0) at once; BACK / Back store them, flush, pop; M3-02 `crt` (`Choice` of `CRT_FILTERS`) and `aspect` (`Choice` of `ASPECT_MODES`), both pushed live as `UserOption` events |
| `ExtrasScene`, `ExtrasItem` | class, const | M3-02, id `extras` — the Options screen's **EXTRAS** page (an overlay, dim `PAUSE_DIM`): the four sim-affecting extras as `Toggle`s — `slowdown`, `graze`, `deathBomb`, `blackHole` (`Slowdown 0, Graze 1, DeathBomb 2, BlackHole 3, Back 4`) — under `APPLIES FROM THE NEXT GAME`, with `BLACK HOLE: THE DIRECT SHIP ONLY` on that row; BACK stores them in `UserOptions.play`, re-arms the next games' configs and writes the save when it changed |
| `ControlsScene` | class | M2-16, id `controls` — the CONTROLS page: `profile` (`Choice` of the profile labels, `DEFAULT` alone and disabled without profiles), `autofire` (`AUTOFIRE_MODES`; disabled on a remote-mode host), `rate` (`AUTOFIRE_INTERVALS` as shots a second), `socd` (the profile's, then `SOCD_CHOICES`), `debounce` (the profile's, then 0–10 ticks), `menu` (`ControlsItem` order), `deviceIndex(pad)`; PROFILE live (`InputProfile` = the choice index), SOCD / DEBOUNCE stored in the save at once and pushed as `InputSettings`; OK on REBIND KEYS / PAD → `rebind.prepare(device)` + push, INPUT TEST → push; BACK / Back store the profile id (when changed), AUTOFIRE / RATE (when changed), re-arm the next games' configs (`applyOptions`), flush, pop. Draws `AUTOFIRE / RATE: FROM THE NEXT GAME` |
| `GameOptionsScene` | class | M2-16, id `gameOptions` — the GAME page: `difficulty` (`Choice`), `lives` (`PRESET`, 1–5), `penalty` (`PRESET`, then `DEATH_PENALTY_PRESETS`), `autoPowerUp`, `magnet`, `oneButton` (`Toggle`s), `menu` (`GameOptionsItem` order), `inGame` (opened over a game in progress — DIFFICULTY disabled, showing the run's); `enter()` shows the save's options (unset toggles: the next game's values); ONE BUTTON disables AUTO POWER and PENALTY while on; BACK / Back store `options.game` (a toggle only when changed; the difficulty kept over a game), `chooseDifficulty` (not over a game), `applyOptions`, flush, pop. Draws `ONE BUTTON: AUTOFIRE, AUTO POWER, CASUAL` and `APPLIES FROM THE NEXT GAME` |
| `RebindScene` | class | M2-16, id `rebind` (over the CONTROLS page): `panel` (the `core/ui` `RebindPanel`), `device`, `releaseTicks`, `prepare(device)` (before the push — the title `KEYBOARD CONTROLS` / `REMOTE CONTROLS` / `GAMEPAD CONTROLS`); `enter()` shows the game context and reads every row's keys from the host; OK on an action → `ControlsSetup.beginCapture` and the prompt; while capturing, `pollCapture()` — `Captured` → `bindCaptured` and the outcome's message (`SHOT REBOUND`, `KEY TAKEN FROM SUB`, `SWAPPED WITH SUB`, `NOT POSSIBLE: … NEEDS A KEY`, `THAT KEY CANNOT BE USED`, `NO CHANGE`), `Cancelled` / `Idle` / `REBIND_CAPTURE_TICKS` → `CANCELLED`; then the rows wait until nothing is held (≤ 60 ticks); RESET → `ControlsSetup.reset`; DONE / Back flush the save, pop; `exit()` ends a running capture. Never allocates per tick |
| `InputTestScene`, `INPUT_TEST_EXIT_TICKS`, `INPUT_TEST_EXIT_PRESSES`, `INPUT_TEST_EXIT_WINDOW_TICKS` | class, consts | M2-16, id `inputTest` (over the CONTROLS page), `inputContext` **`'game'`**: `holdTicks`, `presses`, `pressWindow`, `lit` (the actions held, or pressed within 8 ticks); the device, a direction cross, a box per other game action, `PAUSE X3 OR HOLD TO EXIT` with a bar; **three Pause presses** inside `INPUT_TEST_EXIT_WINDOW_TICKS` (`90`) pop it — M3-02b, the remote's Back / Play-Pause arrive only on release and can never be held — and so does holding Pause `INPUT_TEST_EXIT_TICKS` (`60`); redraws only on a change, never allocates |
| `ControlsItem`, `DisplayItem`, `GameOptionsItem` | const | M2-16: `Profile 0, Autofire 1, Rate 2, Socd 3, Debounce 4, Keys 5, Pad 6, InputTest 7, Back 8` (M3-01: `Rumble 8, Back 9`); `Bullets 0, Scale 1, Shake 2, Flashes 3, Hitbox 4, BossHp 5, Back 6`; `Difficulty 0, Lives 1, Penalty 2, AutoPowerUp 3, Magnet 4, OneButton 5, Back 6` (M3-01: `OptionRecovery 6, Speed 7, Invincible 8, Back 9`); M3-02 `DisplayItem` gained `Crt` and `Aspect` and `OptionsItem` gained `Extras` (the EXTRAS page) |
| `SceneLabels`, `buildSceneLabels(text)` | interface, function | M2-16: the label lists the scenes show (difficulty, bullet palettes, scale modes, flashes, ship models and hints, `!` / `?` / Option choices, the order editor's rows and codes, the ending flags, hi-score modes and ranks, practice loadouts, SFX names, autofire, rates, SOCD, debounce, lives, penalty, contexts, every action's and device kind's name); → frozen lists built from a `UiText` (the flow builds its own once; the exported English label constants are the built-in table's) |
| `StageClearScene` | class | Overlay (dim 0.25): `phase`, `ticks`, `rank` (player 1's place in the saved table, −1 = not entered), since M2-10 `zoneMode` and `next`. A single-stage run: `enter()` records the run (every active player's score — rows with the mode `2p` in a co-op game, M2-06 —, `stagesCleared`, flush); the tally shows `1P` / `2P` scores once player 2 has joined (M2-06); tally `STAGE_CLEAR_TALLY_TICKS` → `TO BE CONTINUED` `STAGE_CLEAR_CONTINUED_TICKS` → `finishGame()` (M2-15: the name entry or the title); OK skips. A campaign zone (M2-10, `zoneMode`): `enter()` tallies (`tallyZone`), pays (`awardZoneBonus`), notes the World for the run, sets `RunFlag.Bonus` after a bonus stage, `captureCarry`s into `run.carry` and picks `next` — the map (counts `stagesCleared`, flush), the ending (`selectCampaignEnding`, the run recorded) or — practice — `finishGame()` (M2-15: its row in the practice table, no `stagesCleared`); the zone tally for `ZONE_TALLY_TICKS`, OK skips |
| `MapScene` | class | M2-10 (shmup_feat.md §14, §17): the zone map, a full screen — `campaign`, `nodeX` / `nodeY` (`Int16Array` node centres: a column per depth between x 40 and 344, rows between y 40 and 136), `exits` (per zone, sorted top to bottom), `edgeDots` (7, or fewer so the edges fit a 160-command budget of the UI list), `ticks`, `launch` (-1 while choosing), `zone` (the cleared zone), `focusedZone`; Up / Down choose an exit (wrap, auto-repeat), OK → `PrepareStage` for its stage and `LAUNCH` for `MAP_LAUNCH_TICKS`, then `advanceZone` + the game; Back → "quit to title?"; entering fades the music out; never allocates per tick or redraw |
| `EndingScene` | class | M2-10 (the ending hook) and M2-14 (shmup_feat.md §15, §17 — the scenes): `ticks`, `phaseTicks`, `phase` (0 the story, 1 the result card), `shown` (epilogue lines shown), `scene` (0 none, 1 citadel, 2 abyss — `run.ending.scene`), `lines` (`run.ending.text`). **Story** (an ending with a scene or text): the sprite scene drawn from `ui/ending-*` and the run's ship(s) — `citadel` (the fortress under chained blasts, falling at tick 480, the ship flying off), `abyss` (the surface's light, bubbles, the ARK's silhouette sinking or — `bossEscaped` — sailing off, the ship rising), a dawn sun for a `noDeath` run — and the epilogue one line every `ENDING_LINE_TICKS` in a panel under it (centred without a scene); OK after `ENDING_LOCK_TICKS` shows every line, OK again or `ENDING_STORY_HOLD_TICKS` after the last → the card. **Result**: `ENDING`, the ending's name (`THE END` without), the route's labels, the score(s) from `run.carry`, a line per run flag set, `THANK YOU FOR PLAYING`, `OK: CREDITS` / `OK: TITLE`; OK after the lock or `ENDING_TIMEOUT_TICKS` → `CreditsScene` when the campaign has credits, else `finishGame()` (M2-15). `enter()` plays the final stage's `music.ending` cue (none = the music goes on). An ending without scene and text opens on the card. Never allocates per tick or redraw |
| `CreditsScene` | class | M2-14 (shmup_feat.md §17), id `credits`: `ticks`, `rows` (the campaign's credits flattened once when the flow is built: a title, its lines, `''` after each section), `scroll` (px scrolled — 1 every `CREDITS_SCROLL_TICKS`, stopping at `scrollEnd`, where the last row stands at y 100), `scrollEnd`, `stopped`. Rows scroll up from y 216 at `CREDITS_ROW_HEIGHT`, titles in the title colour; only the rows on screen are drawn, through `CREDITS_STRING_SLOTS` slots taken by row number; OK / Back after `CREDITS_LOCK_TICKS`, or the end of `CREDITS_HOLD_TICKS` after the stop → `finishGame()` (M2-15: the name entry or the title). `enter()` plays the final stage's `music.credits` cue. Never allocates per tick or redraw |
| `RunState` | class | M2-10 (`scenes/run.ts`): `campaign`, `zone`, `stage`, `depth`, `route`, `practice`, `checkpoint`, `loadout` (M2-15: a practice run's starting loadout, `null` = the config's), `pendingStart`, `entry` / `carry` (`CarryState`), `inBonus`, `bonusStage`, `bonusReturnX`, `bonusLocked`, `flags`, `deaths`, `continues`, `continuesAtStart`, `result` (`ZoneResult`), `ending`; getters `endingFlags` (+ `NoDeath` / `NoContinue` when none) and `finalZone`; `begin(campaign, stage)`, `beginPractice(campaign, zone, checkpoint, loadout = null)`, `advance(zone)` (the carry becomes the entry), `leaveBonus(lock)`, `noteWorldEnd(world)` (deaths, continues used, `BossEscaped`). M3-01: `mode` (`RunMode`), `loop`, `timeLimit`, `assists` (the run's `AssistFlag`s), `segmentStart` (`WorldStart` of the World prepared last); `begin(campaign, stage, mode = 'normal', loop = 1, timeLimit = 0)`, `beginZone(campaign, zone, mode, timeLimit)` (the CARAVAN: one zone, not practice), `nextLoop()` (the ARCADE: the first zone again, one loop higher, the zone count restarting). M3-02: `inEscape` and `escapeStage` (the final zone's escape sequence — `CampaignZoneSpec.escape`; a World on that stage with the run's carry and no caravan clock, then the ending). The route, the zone count and the hi-score row's `reached` are unchanged by it |
| `RunMode`, `RUN_MODES` | type, const | M3-01 (shmup_feat.md §16): `'normal' \| 'bossRush' \| 'caravan' \| 'arcade'`; the four, frozen |
| `WorldStart`, `prepareWorldStart(world, start, carry)` | class, function | M3-01 (`scenes/run.ts`): what the flow puts into a new World before its first tick — `stageTerm` (-1 = keep), `returnX` (a bonus entrance to jump to, -1), `checkpoint` (-1), `locked`; applies it and the carried players at tick 0 (what `prepareRunWorld` and a run replay's playback both run). Cold path |
| `RunRecorder` | class | M3-01 (`scenes/replays.ts`; `SceneFlow.recorder`): `segment` (a `core/replay` `SegmentRecorder`), `segments`, `valid`, `recording`; `beginRun()`, `cancel()`, `beginSegment(world, previous, start, carry, buildId, godMode, assists)` (ends the previous World's segment, starts this World's: its header and `worldStartJson` of its start), `endSegment(world, assists)`, `invalidate()`, `record(input)` / `check(world)` (every game tick — never allocate), `action(code, param)`, `seal(world)` (before the zone tally), `finishRun(world, meta) → RunReplay \| null` (`null` when invalid, overflowed or empty) |
| `RunReplayMeta` | interface | M3-01: `{ buildId, mode, label, score, reached, assists }` — what `finishRun` writes into the `RunReplay` |
| `RunReplayPlayback`, `RunPlaybackReport` | classes | M3-01: `new RunReplayPlayback(run, content, events = createEventQueue())` — `run`, `report`, `world` (the segment's, `null` between), `flags` (its own debug switches; god mode from each header), `running`, `config`, `nextStage` (the stage the next segment starts on — prepare its music), `nextSegment()` (creates the World from the header, applies the start state and the tick-0 actions), `step()` (one recorded tick, the actions due between ticks, every hash compared) → still running; the report: `ok`, `checked`, `desyncSegment` / `desyncTick` of the first mismatch (-1), `finished` |
| `worldStartJson(start, carry, hiScore)`, `readWorldStart(json)`, `ReadWorldStart` | functions, interface | M3-01: a World's start state as plain JSON (the stage term, return x, checkpoint, lock, the session hi-score and every carried player with its loadout and whole shield) and back — `readWorldStart` → `{ start, carry, hiScore }`, throws `RangeError` for a missing or malformed field |
| `CarryState`, `CarriedPlayer` | classes | M2-10: every player's carried state (`valid`, `players`, `continuesUsed`, `hiScore`; `copyFrom(from)`) — per player `active`, `lives`, `out`, `down`, `score`, `continues`, `nextExtend`, `extendsEarned`, `main`, `missile`, `options`, `shot`, `sub`, `family`, `speedLevel`, `cursor`, `shield` (a private `ShieldState`) |
| `captureCarry(world, out)`, `applyCarry(world, carry)`, `copyShieldState(from, to)` | functions | M2-10, cold paths: take the players out of a World (→ `out`, now valid); put them into a new World at tick 0 (an out player dead with 0 lives, a down one respawning, the rest flying in; hi-score, continues used, rank and view refreshed; an invalid carry changes nothing); copy a shield's lasting state (the timers start fresh) |
| `runWorldConfig(base, run)`, `prepareRunWorld(world, run, carry)` | functions | M2-10: the World's config — `base` (since M2-16 the flow's `runConfig`, taken when the run began) with the zone's (or the bonus stage's) stage and, in a practice run, the run's loadout (M2-15), `base` itself when neither differs; prepare a new World — `rankInputs.stage = depth + 1` in a campaign, the jump back to a failed bonus stage's entrance (else a practice checkpoint), the entrances' lock, the carry (→ `world`) |
| `ZoneResult`, `tallyZone(world, out, bonus = false)`, `awardZoneBonus(world, result)`, `worldDeaths(world)` | class, functions | M2-10: `spawned`, `killed`, `killPercent`, `killBonus`, `bossSeconds` (-1 without a defeated stage boss), `timeBonus`, `bonus`; the tally of a cleared World (→ `out`); pays kill + time bonus to every player in play and checks the extends (→ the points each got); the ships lost (sum of `hits`) |
| `RunFlag`, `KILL_BONUS_PER_PERCENT`, `TIME_BONUS_PAR_TICKS`, `TIME_BONUS_PER_SECOND` | const | M2-10: `BossEscaped 1`, `NoDeath 2`, `NoContinue 4`, `Bonus 8` (= `core/data` `RUN_FLAG_NAMES` bits); `100`; `5400` (90 s); `100` |
| `BONUS_WARP_TICKS`, `BONUS_FAIL_TICKS`, `ZONE_CARD_TICKS`, `ZONE_TALLY_TICKS`, `MAP_LAUNCH_TICKS`, `ENDING_LOCK_TICKS`, `ENDING_TIMEOUT_TICKS` | const | M2-10: `40`, `60`, `150`, `300`, `60`, `60`, `1200` (since M2-14 counted from the result card) |
| `ENDING_LINE_TICKS`, `ENDING_STORY_HOLD_TICKS`, `CREDITS_SCROLL_TICKS`, `CREDITS_ROW_HEIGHT`, `CREDITS_LOCK_TICKS`, `CREDITS_HOLD_TICKS`, `CREDITS_STRING_SLOTS` | const | M2-14: `90` (ticks between epilogue lines), `240` (the whole epilogue's hold), `2` (ticks per pixel of scroll — 30 px/s), `11` (px a row), `60`, `240`, `24` |
| `GameOverScene` | class | Overlay (dim 0.35): `ticks`, `rank`; `enter()` records the run (scores, `gameOvers` — not for practice —, flush) and the panel shows `NEW HI-SCORE` below it when `rank` (player 1's) is 0 — and both players' scores (`1P` / `2P`) once player 2 has joined (M2-06); OK / Back after `GAME_OVER_LOCK_TICKS`, or `GAME_OVER_TIMEOUT_TICKS` → `finishGame()` (M2-15: the name entry when a score entered its table, else the title) |
| `NameEntryScene` | class | M2-15 (shmup_feat.md §17), id `nameEntry`: `entry` (a `core/ui` `NameEntry`), `current` (the pending row being named), `ticks`, `rank`, `seconds`; `enter()` plays the title theme and opens the first pending row still in its table (`recordRun` kept them by object identity); letters / cursor / `END` through `nameEntryTick` (the four directions and OK only); on `done` or `NAME_ENTRY_TIMEOUT_TICKS` the row is renamed (`SaveStore.renameScore`) and the next pending row opens — rows pushed out of their table are skipped —, then the save is flushed and `showResults()`. Never allocates per tick or redraw (a finished name builds its string and a new row) |
| `HiScoreScene` | class | M2-15 (shmup_feat.md §15, §17), id `hiScore`: `mode` (0 attract, 1 result), `pages` (mode keys, built on `enter`), `page`, `ticks`, `key` (getter: the table showing); `showAttract()` / `showResult(key)` before it is pushed. Attract: the chosen ship and difficulty's 1P table, then every other table with rows (`POWER_UP_MODES` × `DIFFICULTY_PRESETS` × `HI_SCORE_MODES`), at most `HI_SCORE_ATTRACT_PAGES`, `HI_SCORE_PAGE_TICKS` each, then `nextAttract` (the story or the title); any press → title. Result: the game's table with its new rows blinking (`isNewRow`), OK / Back after `HI_SCORE_LOCK_TICKS` or `HI_SCORE_RESULT_TICKS` → title. Rows: `HI_SCORE_RANK_LABELS`, name, score, the zone's campaign label (`-` otherwise); the title line `KESTREL  NORMAL  1 PLAYER` (`tableTitle`); the title theme; 37 string slots |
| `DemoScene` | class | M2-15 (shmup_feat.md §16), id `demo`: `demo` (the `core/replay` `DemoPlayback` playing, or `null`), `world` (getter), `started` (demos started — the next is `started % demos`), `ticks`, `hud` / `hudList` (its own HUD — the flow's frame shows the demo World and this HUD while the scene is visible); `enter()` fades the music out and starts the next demo (`createDemoPlayback` into a private queue — a transition; a demo that cannot start ends at once), `exit()` drops the World; `tick()`: any press → title, else one recorded tick and the private queue drained into the session's **except** sounds, music, ducking, rumble and host requests (silent); the recording's end or a desync → `nextAttract` (the tables). `DEMO PLAY` / `PRESS OK` alternate, the zone card shows 180 ticks. Never allocates per tick (the spawns' coroutines aside, D29) |
| `StoryScene` | class | M2-15 (shmup_feat.md §17), id `story`: `rows` (the campaign's story pages flattened when the flow is built, a blank row after each page), `ticks`, `scroll` (px — 1 every `STORY_SCROLL_TICKS`), `page` (the page whose scene plays: the last whose first row crawled half-way up the panel), `duration` (every row through the panel + `STORY_HOLD_TICKS`); the rows inside the panel drawn through `STORY_STRING_SLOTS` slots taken by row number; the scenes `dawn` / `invasion` / `launch` drawn from `ui/ending-*`, the ships and the logo; the end → title; any press → title. Never allocates per tick or redraw |
| `PracticeScene` | class | M2-15 (shmup_feat.md §16), id `practice`: overlay, dim `PAUSE_DIM`, an opaque 240×116 panel — `zone` (`Choice`: `A AZURE VERGE` …), `checkpoint` (`START`, `CHECKPOINT 1` … up to the busiest zone's count), `loadout` (`PRACTICE_LOADOUT_LABELS`), `menu` (`PracticeItem` order), `checkpoints` (per zone the stage checkpoint indices after x 0); a change keeps CHECKPOINT within the focused zone's (wrapping both ways; a zone change takes its last); START stores the choice (`FlowControl.practice`), chooses one player and pushes the difficulty menu — the chain's last OK (`launchGame()`) starts the practice run; Back → the mode select. Never allocates per tick |
| `SoundTestScene` | class | M2-15 (shmup_feat.md §21), id `soundTest`: overlay, dim `PAUSE_DIM`, an opaque 256×112 panel — `music` (`Choice` of the host's titles, `NONE` and disabled without), `sound` (`Choice` of `SFX_TEST_LABELS`), `menu` (`SoundTestItem` order); OK on MUSIC pushes `SimEventKind.SoundTest` (the index), on SFX an `Sfx` event at x `PLAYFIELD_W / 2` (centred), masked out of the input `menuTick` reads so it does not step the choice; STOP → `Music Silence`; BACK / Back → the title theme, pop. ### `save` — versioned saves, hi-score tables

The persisted save (M1-17): options, hi-score tables and stats as one versioned JSON document
under `Platform.storage`, with migrations, defensive parsing and write-on-change. Pure — the
storage comes in as a `PlatformStorage`. Guide: [saves-and-options.md](saves-and-options.md).

| Export | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `SaveData` | interface | `{ version, options: UserOptions, hiScores: Record<modeKey, HiScoreEntry[≤ 10]>, stats: SaveStats }` — frozen; `options.display.bulletPalette` since M2-02, `scaleMode`, `screenShake`, `reduceFlashing`, `showHitbox` since M2-08, `bossHpBar` since M2-09 (version 1: missing display fields resolve to their defaults); since M2-16 (version 2) `options.input.autofire`, `autofireInterval`, `socd`, `releaseDebounce`, `bindings` and `options.game`; since M3-01 (still version 2 — the new fields resolve when missing) `options.play` (`PlayOptions`), a row's `assisted` (written only when `true`) and `unlocks?` (`SaveUnlocks`, written only once something is unlocked) |
| `SaveStats` | interface | `gamesStarted` (START and RETRY STAGE), `gameOvers`, `stagesCleared` — whole numbers, capped at 2³¹−1 |
| `HiScoreEntry` | type | Re-exported from `scoring` |
| `SAVE_VERSION`, `SAVE_STORAGE_KEY`, `SAVE_CORRUPT_KEY` | const | `2` (since M2-16; `1` before); `'save.v1'` (the adapters prefix it: `shmup-cup:save.v1`; the key stays for the whole format family — the document's `version` drives migrations); `'save.corrupt'` (where a corrupt or unreadable text is copied) |
| `HI_SCORE_TABLE_SIZE`, `DEFAULT_HI_SCORE_NAME`, `HI_SCORE_NAME_MAX`, `MAX_HI_SCORE_TABLES` | const | `10`; `'---'` (a row before its name entry finishes — M2-15 — or a blank name); `8`; `64` since M3-01 (`32` before) |
| `HI_SCORE_MODES`, `HiScoreMode` | const, type | M2-15 (shmup_feat.md §15, §16): `'1p'`, `'2p'`, `'practice'` — the game modes with tables of their own; also the rows' `mode` field; M3-01 appended `'bossrush'`, `'caravan'`, `'arcade'` (the EXTRA modes' tables) |
| `SaveUnlocks`, `UNLOCK_IDS`, `UnlockId` | interface, const, type | M3-01 (shmup_feat.md §21 unlocks, §7A, §16): `{ extraEdit, loop2 }` — set by reaching an ending (both) or the title's EXTRA EDIT code, never taken back; `['extraEdit', 'loop2']` |
| `SaveMigration`, `SAVE_MIGRATIONS` | interface, const | `{ from, to, migrate(data) }` (must not throw for any input); frozen steps, `SAVE_MIGRATIONS[n]` = version n → n + 1. Step 0 → 1 reads the pre-release layout (flat `hiScores` list → the `meter-normal` table, float volumes 0–1 → levels, `profile` → `input.profileId`, `unlocks` dropped). Step 1 → 2 (M2-16, `migrateV1`) adds the controls fields (`null`, `bindings: {}`) and `options.game` (every field `null`, `oneButton: false`) and moves the co-op / practice rows of the one-player tables into their `-2p` / `-practice` tables (ahead of the rows there — older rows win ties). Append a step and bump `SAVE_VERSION` for a new format; never edit a shipped one |
| `createDefaultSave()` | function | → a frozen document: `DEFAULT_USER_OPTIONS`, no tables, zero stats |
| `migrateSave(data, migrations = SAVE_MIGRATIONS)` | function | → `{ data, fromVersion }` (not yet sanitised; no `version` = 0); throws `RangeError` for a version that is not a non-negative integer, newer than `SAVE_VERSION`, or without a step |
| `sanitizeSave(data)` | function | → a frozen, valid `SaveData` from a migrated document — never throws: options through `resolveUserOptions`; only lower-case kebab mode keys ≤ 32 characters, ≤ 32 tables in key order; rows need a finite score ≥ 0 (floored, capped at `MAX_SCORE`), texts cut, tables sorted best first (stable) and cut to 10, empty ones dropped (M2-15 moved rows of mode `2p` / `practice` out of the one-player tables here on every read; since M2-16 the version-1 → 2 migration does it once); stats whole numbers ≥ 0 (never `-0`); unknown fields dropped |
| `parseSave(text, migrations?)`, `ParsedSave`, `SaveStatus` | function, interface, type | JSON → migrations → sanitiser, never throws → `{ data, status, fromVersion, reason }`; `status` `'empty'` (null text), `'ok'`, `'migrated'`, `'corrupt'` (not JSON / not an object → defaults), `'unreadable'` (bad or newer version, a missing or throwing migration → defaults) |
| `serializeSave(data)` | function | → compact JSON, **canonical**: fixed field order, tables in key order, rows field by field, (M2-16) the bindings by profile id, `game` before `menu`, actions in `ACTION_NAMES` order — equal documents give equal text however they were built |
| `loadSave(storage)`, `LoadedSave` | function, interface | → `Promise<LoadedSave>` (`ParsedSave` + `text`); never rejects (a failing `get` = empty); a corrupt / unreadable text is copied to `save.corrupt` (awaited, best effort); the main key is left for the next flush |
| `writeSave(storage, data)` | function | Writes unconditionally → `Promise<void>`; rejects when the storage rejects. Hosts use `SaveStore.flush` |
| `createHiScoreEntry(score, fields?)` | function | → a frozen row: score floored and capped (negative / non-finite → 0), name cut to 8 (missing / empty → `---`), `reached` / `mode` / `difficulty` cut to 32 (missing → `''`) |
| `insertHiScore(table, entry)`, `HiScoreInsert` | function, interface | → `{ table, rank }` — a new table (the input unchanged; the same array when the row did not enter) and the rank (0 = best, −1): a row enters when the table has < 10 rows or it beats the 10th; ties go below the older rows; a score of 0 never enters |
| `hiScoreModeKey(config, mode = '1p')` | function | → `<powerUpMode>-<difficulty>` for one player (`'meter-normal'` in M1; since M2-01 each difficulty has its own table: `meter-easy`, `meter-normal`, `meter-hard`, `meter-arcade`; since M2-05 the Direct-mode MANTA's games go into `direct-easy` … `direct-arcade`), since M2-15 with `-2p` for a co-op game and `-practice` for a practice run (`direct-normal-2p`, `meter-easy-practice`) — one table per difficulty × ship (the power-up model) × mode |
| `parseHiScoreModeKey(key)`, `HiScoreKeyParts` | function, interface | M2-15: → `{ powerUpMode, difficulty, mode }` of a key `hiScoreModeKey` makes, or `null` for another shape (an explicit `-1p` included) — the hi-score screen's title line |
| `SaveStore`, `createSaveStore(storage, loaded?)` | class, function | The document the game plays with: `storage` (`null` = memory only), `data`, `options`, `dirty` (serialises — not for hot paths), `writes`; `setOptions(options)` (sanitised), `hiScores(modeKey)` (empty table when none), `bestScore(modeKey)`, `recordScore(modeKey, entry)` → rank or −1 (bad mode key, a 33rd table, not entered), `renameScore(modeKey, entry, name)` (M2-15: names the row the table holds — found by object identity, so rows recorded after it cannot misplace it — with a renamed copy → its rank, or −1 when the table no longer holds it; the name cut to 8, empty → `---`), `count(stat)` (stops at 2³¹−1), `replace(data)` (M2-17 — the debug save import: the whole document, sanitised again with `sanitizeSave`) — all in memory until `flush()` → `Promise<boolean>` (writes only when the canonical text differs from the stored one; `true` = written; never rejects; a failed write makes the next flush write). `createSaveStore` counts the loaded text as stored only for status `'ok'`. M3-01: `unlocked(id)` → whether an `UnlockId` is set; `unlock(id)` → `true` when it was locked (written by the next flush) |

### `replay` — recording, playback and desync detection

Implemented with M1-19 (`shmup_feat.md` §21); attract playback since M2-15 (§16). Since M2-15 the
session-free parts live in `replay/format.ts` (no `core/game` import — `core/scenes` uses it) and
the attract playback in `replay/demo.ts`; `replay/index.ts` keeps `createReplayGame` / `playReplay`
and re-exports both; since M3-01 `replay/run.ts` holds the whole-run replays, their segment
recorder, the assist flags and the replay library (re-exported too). Guides:
[debug-and-replays.md](debug-and-replays.md#replays-corereplay),
[front-end-and-attract.md](front-end-and-attract.md#attract-playback-corereplay),
[extra-modes-and-replays.md](extra-modes-and-replays.md#whole-run-replays-corereplay-runts).

| Export | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `createReplayHeader(config, { buildId?, checkpoint?, assisted?, assists? })` | function | → frozen `ReplayHeader` (`buildId` default `'dev'`, `checkpoint` −1, `assisted` false); throws `RangeError` for a checkpoint that is not an integer ≥ −1; M3-01: `assists` defaults to `GodMode` when `assisted` plus `Invincible` when `config.invincible`, and throws `RangeError` when it is not a mask 0–255 |
| `ReplayHeader`, `ReplayHeaderOptions` | interfaces | `formatVersion`, `buildId`, `seed`, `config` (the whole resolved `GameConfig` — since M2-01 with every difficulty-preset value, so a replay does not depend on the content's `rules` table; since M2-16 with `autofireMode` — the save's options come in only through it), `stageId`, `checkpoint`, `loadout`, `assisted` (god mode for the whole run); the options above; M3-01: `assists` (the run's `AssistFlag`s — informative; a replay recorded before it decodes as `GodMode` when `assisted`, else 0) |
| `Replay` | interface | `header`, `ticks`, `inputs` (`MAX_PLAYERS` `Uint32Array`s of `held \| pressed << 16`), `hashInterval`, `hashes` (after ticks `k · interval`), `finalHash` |
| `packReplayInput(held, pressed)` | function | → `(held & 0xffff) \| (pressed & 0xffff) << 16`, unsigned |
| `createReplayRecorder(source, header, { capacity?, hashInterval? })` | function | → `ReplayRecorder` (a `PlatformInput` wrapping `source`): `poll()` records every player's masks and returns the source's snapshot; `check(world)` after every tick (stores `hashWorld` on interval ticks); `finish(world)` → the `Replay` (throws `Error` when a periodic hash is missing); `header`, `ticks`. Preallocates `capacity` ticks (36,000), doubles beyond; throws `RangeError` for a non-positive capacity / interval |
| `RecorderOptions`, `ReplayRecorder` | interfaces | Above |
| `createPlayback(replay, { buildId? })` | function | → `ReplayPlayback` (a `PlatformInput`): `poll()` → the next recorded tick (*reused* snapshot, `released` derived, device `'none'`, idle after the end); `check(world)` compares hashes on interval ticks and after the last; `replay`, `ticks`, `done`, `report` |
| `ReplayPlayback`, `PlaybackOptions`, `DesyncReport` | interfaces | `DesyncReport { ok, checked, desyncTick (−1), expectedHash, actualHash, finished, buildMatches (null without a build id) }` — the first mismatch is kept |
| `createReplayGame(platform, header, content)` | function | → the `Game` the header describes (bare gameplay, `debug.godMode = assisted`, `jumpToCheckpoint` for a checkpoint ≥ 0) — the same setup for recording and playback; throws `RangeError` for a bad config, unknown stage or missing checkpoint |
| `playReplay(replay, content, options?)` | function | Plays a whole replay headless → `ReplayRun { report, game }` |
| `ReplayRun` | interface | Above |
| `encodeReplay(replay)`, `decodeReplay(data)` | functions | ↔ `ReplayJson { kind: 'replay', header, ticks, hashInterval, inputs (base64 RLE per player), hashes, finalHash }`; `decodeReplay` validates everything and throws `RangeError` naming the problem (extra top-level fields ignored, unknown config keys dropped, missing ones defaulted — an M1 header without the M2-01 fields gets its preset's values from the built-in table, a header without `autofireMode` gets `'always'`) |
| `ReplayJson` | interface | Above |
| `encodeInputRuns(words, ticks?)`, `decodeInputRuns(text, ticks)` | functions | Runs of `(value, count)` as unsigned LEB128 varints, base64; decoding throws `RangeError` for truncated / over-long varints, empty runs, runs not adding up to `ticks` |
| `encodeBase64(bytes)`, `decodeBase64(text)` | functions | Standard alphabet, `=` padding, no `btoa`; decoding throws `RangeError` for a bad length, character or padding |
| `REPLAY_KIND`, `REPLAY_FORMAT_VERSION`, `REPLAY_HASH_INTERVAL` | const | `'replay'`, `1`, `600` |
| `createDemoPlayback(replay, content, { events? })` | function | M2-15 (`replay/demo.ts`): → a `DemoPlayback` before its first tick — the World the header describes (`createWorld` with the config re-resolved over the content's difficulty table, god mode from `assisted` in the World's **own** `DebugFlags`, `jumpToCheckpoint` for a checkpoint ≥ 0), pushing into `events` (default a new private queue), and the replay's playback; throws `RangeError` for a stage the content lacks or a missing checkpoint |
| `DemoPlayback` | class | M2-15: `replay`, `world`, `playback` (its `report` updated after every tick), `flags`; `running` (getter: recorded ticks left and every hash matched so far), `step()` → still running (polls the recording, `stepWorld`, checks a due hash; nothing once over — a desync ends the demo). Never allocates per step (the stage's spawns' coroutines aside) |
| `DemoPlaybackOptions`, `DEMO_BUILD_ID` | interface, const | M2-15: `{ events? }`; `'demo'` — the build id the bundled demos are recorded with (hashes, not a build, lock them) |
| `RunReplay`, `RunSegment`, `RunReplayJson`, `RunSegmentJson` | interfaces | M3-01 (`replay/run.ts`, shmup_feat.md §21): a whole run through the scene flow — `formatVersion`, `buildId`, `mode` (its hi-score mode), `label` (`KESTREL NORMAL`), `score`, `reached` (the last zone's label), `assists`, `ticks`, `segments`; a segment is one World: `replay` (an M1-19 `Replay` with the World's header), `start` (the flow's start state JSON, or `null`), `actions` (`Uint32Array` of `[tick, code \| param << 8]` pairs); the JSON forms (`kind: 'run-replay'`, actions as a number list) |
| `encodeRunReplay(run)`, `decodeRunReplay(data)`, `runReplayText(run)`, `parseRunReplayText(text)` | functions | M3-01: ↔ `RunReplayJson`; `decodeRunReplay` throws `RangeError` (wrong kind or version, bad score / assists, 0 or more than `MAX_RUN_SEGMENTS` segments, a segment that fails `decodeReplay`, a start that is not an object or `null`, actions that are not ordered `[tick, code]` pairs within the segment); → compact JSON text; → the replay or `null` (never throws) |
| `RUN_REPLAY_KIND`, `RUN_REPLAY_FORMAT_VERSION`, `SEGMENT_CAPACITY`, `MAX_RUN_SEGMENTS`, `MAX_SEGMENT_ACTIONS` | const | M3-01: `'run-replay'`, `1`, `72_000` ticks a World (20 minutes), `48`, `64` |
| `SegmentRecorder` | class | M3-01: records one World of a run — `new SegmentRecorder(capacity = SEGMENT_CAPACITY)`; `header`, `start`, `ticks`, `finalHash`, `sealed`, `overflow`, `actionCount`, `active`; `begin(header, start)`, `reset()`, `record(input)` (before each `stepWorld`), `check(world)` (after it — the hash every `REPLAY_HASH_INTERVAL` ticks), `action(code, param)`, `seal(world)` (the final hash now), `finish(world, assists) → RunSegment \| null` (`null` when idle or overflowed). `record` / `check` / `action` never allocate |
| `RunAction` | const + type | M3-01: `Continue 1` (param = the `who` mask), `FullPower 2`, `SelfDestruct 3` (param = the slot) — what the flow does to a World between ticks |
| `AssistFlag`, `runAssisted(assists)` | const, function | M3-01 (shmup_feat.md §21 "flag scores/replays as assisted"): `GodMode 1`, `Invincible 2`, `Speed 4`, `Secret 8`; → `true` when any is set |
| `ReplayLibrary`, `createReplayLibrary(storage)`, `ReplaySummary`, `ReplayStoreResult` | interface, function, interface, const + type | M3-01: the saved replays over `Platform.storage` (`null` = memory only): `storage`, `summaries` (per slot `{ slot, mode, label, score, reached, assisted, ticks }` or `null`), `revision`, `load()` (never rejects; an unreadable, invalid or oversize slot is empty and an oversize one's key cleared), `replay(slot)`, `exportText(slot)`, `storeLast(run)` → `Ok` / `TooLong`, `keep(slot)` → the kept slot or -1, `importText(text)` → `Ok` / `TooLong` / `Invalid` / `Full`, `remove(slot)`; a slot that is not a whole number 0–3 is empty. `ReplayStoreResult`: `Ok 0`, `TooLong 1`, `Invalid 2`, `Full 3` |
| `replayStorageKey(slot)`, `REPLAY_SLOTS`, `KEPT_REPLAY_SLOTS`, `MAX_REPLAY_TEXT`, `MAX_KEPT_REPLAY_TEXT` | function, const | M3-01: `replay.last` (slot 0 — the last game), `replay.1` … `replay.3`; `4`; `3`; `120_000` characters a replay (240,042 bytes with the key in the web / Tizen adapter — under its 256 KiB a value); `130_000` for the kept slots together (the library ≤ 500,150 bytes, so the save and its corrupt copy still fit the 1 MiB budget) |

### `module-info`

`defineModule({ name, status, specRefs })` → frozen `ModuleInfo`; `ModuleStatus` =
`'placeholder' | 'partial' | 'implemented'`. Every module exports one as `moduleInfo`.

### Placeholder modules

None is left in `@shmup/core`: modules joined `src/index.ts` as they were implemented — `rng`,
`math`, `events` and `pools` in M1-01, `world`, `player`, `collision` and `debug` in M1-06,
`stage` in M1-07, `enemies`, `patterns` and the new `behaviors` in M1-08, `bullets` and `rank` in
M1-09 (`rank` implemented with M2-01), `weapons` and `options` in M1-10, `powerups` and `shields` in M1-11, `scoring` and `fx` in
M1-12, `bosses` in M1-13, `ui` and `scenes` in M1-16, `save` (with the `config` user options) in
M1-17 and `replay` in M1-19 (the `exports` map still has only `"."`).

Still planned inside the partial modules: `config` — the M3 assists (game speed, invincibility — M3-01), the visual & mechanic extras (M3-02) and a language option (M3-03); the option groups are complete since M2-16 (the bullet palette came with M2-02, the scale mode, shake, flashing and hitbox options with M2-08, the boss HP bar with M2-09, the controls and game options and the saved difficulty with M2-16 — the weapon select's loadout and the ship stay session-only); `save` (implemented) — unlocks (the Electron file store came with M2-17, in `apps/electron`; save v2 with the M2-16 options came with M2-16, the names of the name entry and
the per-mode tables with M2-15); `player` (implemented for P0; co-op joining and continuing live in `world` since M2-06); `scoring` — nothing co-op is left (per-player scores, extends and continues since M2-06; the Direct-mode 1UP item of M2-05 lives in `powerups`); `fx` — nothing left (the authentic slowdown of M3-02 became the World's tick skip, not an `fx` timer); `collision` (implemented since M2-07: moving blocks and destructible tiles); `debug` (implemented; the overlay's device line came with M2-17, in `@shmup/render-pixi` /
`@shmup/shell`); `replay` (implemented; attract playback since M2-15) — recording the scene flow, save /
share / fast-forward (M3-01); `stage`
(implemented; holds, diagonal pans, branches, region triggers and the stage gimmicks since M2-07,
following a raid's camera target since M2-09, the hidden bonus-stage entrances since M2-10) —
nothing left for the zones' own entrances (zone B's `gap` came with M2-11 as content, zone G's
`ground` with M2-13);
`enemies` — nothing left for the zones (M2-14 added the proximity wake `sleepUntilNear`; zones B–G needed nothing new); `behaviors` (implemented since M2-14 — the roster of all nine zones: B and C's came with M2-11, D and E's with M2-12, F and G's with M2-13, H and I's with M2-14);
`bosses` (implemented since M2-09: four slots, turned parts, captains, raids, boss-inside-boss,
double bosses, timers and escapes, the HP bar's model, boss rush) — boss behaviours running DSL
patterns (the zones' bosses are done: `restX` / `restY` came with M2-11, zones D–G needed no engine change — M2-12, M2-13 —, the spiral stream with M2-14); `bullets` (implemented; graze and the black hole's vortex came with M3-02) — DSL-fired lasers and
boss / revenge DSL patterns (with the zones' bosses); `rank` (implemented; the campaign sets the
stage term since M2-10, the loop term since M3-01); `weapons` (implemented — both models since M2-05, both players' loadouts in co-op since M2-06); `options` (implemented since M2-04; option recovery after death came with M3-01); `powerups` (implemented since M2-05) — the yellow item's damage to mid-bosses (with the zones' captains; M2-09 brought the captains, not the damage) (co-op item ownership and drop scaling came with M2-06); `shields` (implemented — the Arm since M2-05); `scenes` (implemented since M2-15 — the mode select, the attract loop, the name entry, the hi-score
tables, the practice select and the sound test; M2-10 brought the zone map, the zone tally and the
ending hook, M2-14 the ending scenes and the credits, M2-16 the Options pages, the rebind screen and
the input test) — recording the flow in replays (M3-01); `ui` — the language choice and more font
glyphs (M3-03) (the Direct-mode tier pips came with M2-05, the co-op halves with M2-06, the boss HP
bar with M2-09, the name entry with M2-15, the rebind widget and the string table with M2-16).

## `@shmup/input-web`

Keyboard / remote / gamepad → the core's `InputSnapshot`, driven by the data-driven input
profiles of `content/input/` (decisions D13–D15). Guides: [input-profiles.md](input-profiles.md),
[coop.md](coop.md#input-routing-shmupinput-web-shmupshell) (player seats and the split keyboard, M2-06),
[options-rebinding-and-accessibility.md](options-rebinding-and-accessibility.md#rebinding-shmupinput-web-rebind) (the rebinding and its capture, M2-16).

| Export | Module | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `createWebInput({ keyTarget, getGamepads?, bindings?, keyDevice? })` | `web-input` | → `WebInput`. `poll()` once per tick (ages the debounce first); *reused* snapshot. Devices are routed to players by **seats** (M2-06; `implemented`): one seat — every device, pads and both halves of a split keyboard included, drives player 1; two seats (a co-op game) — the keyboard / remote is player 1's, the split keyboard's right half player 2's, and an unseated pad drives player 1 until its first join press (a button the gamepad profile's **menu** table binds to Confirm or Pause) seats it as player 2 and is forwarded as a latched `Confirm` there. `poll()`, `setContext()`, `setSeats()` and the event handlers never allocate |
| `WebInput` | `web-input` | `PlatformInput` + `keyboard`, `context` (`'game'` initially), `keyProfile`, `gamepadProfile` (`null` = built-in defaults), `setProfile(profile)` (key profiles → keyboard source and reported device; gamepad profiles → every pad), `setContext(ctx)` (no-op when unchanged; remembered without a profile), `clear()`, `destroy()`; co-op (M2-06): `splitKeyboard` (player 2's half of a split key profile), `seats` (1 or 2), `setSeats(count)` (the host forwards `Game.inputSeats`; allocation-free; a source that changes players keeps what it holds there without a new press), `padSeat(index)` (a pad that disappears gives its seat up on that poll); the rebinding capture (M2-16): `beginCapture(kind)` (`'keys'` — the next new key of the keyboard / remote, or `'buttons'` — the lowest newly pressed button of any pad; keys and buttons already held do not count; the captured press is also handled as usual), `capture` (`InputCaptureState`, *reused* — updated in `poll()`), `endCapture()` (back to `Idle`); M3-01: `rumble(player, strength)` → pads rumbled (two seats: player 2's seated pad, every other connected pad player 1's; one seat: every pad for player 1, none for player 2) through `rumblePad` |
| `CaptureKind`, `InputCaptureState` | `web-input` | M2-16: `'keys' \| 'buttons'`; a class `{ status (a core/ui CaptureStatus), kind, code ('' for a TV remote key or a button), keyCode (0 for a button), button (-1 for a key) }` — Escape (`code` `Escape`) and the remote's Back (keyCode 10009) set `Cancelled`; a key during a button capture is ignored |
| `WebInputOptions` | `web-input` | `keyTarget`, `getGamepads?`, `bindings?` and `keyDevice?` (used until a profile is applied) |
| `PAD_SEAT_NONE` (`-1`), `PAD_SEAT_P2` (`1`) | `web-input` | `WebInput.padSeat(index)` results: the pad has no seat (drives player 1) / holds player 2's seat |
| `createKeyboardSource(target \| null, bindings, tuning = DEFAULT_INPUT_TUNING)` | `keyboard` | → `KeyboardSource`: `held` (tracked keys, SOCD + diagonal policy applied; computed on read), `bindings`, `tuning`, `consumeLatched()`, `advance()` (one debounce poll per tick), `setBindings(table)` (held keys keep only actions common to both tables), `setTuning(t)`, `clear()`, `handleEvent(e)`, `detach()` |
| `KeyEventLike` | `keyboard` | The `KeyboardEvent` fields read (tests pass plain objects) |
| `KeyCapture`, `KeyboardSource.capture` | `keyboard` | M2-16: a class `{ armed, count, code, keyCode }`; while `armed`, the next keydown of a key not already down (auto-repeats, a held key and a key inside its release debounce — a remote's fake keyup / keydown pair — do not count) is caught, bound or not, `preventDefault()`-ed (Ctrl / Cmd shortcuts excepted) and then handled as usual; `count` increases per catch |
| `MAX_TRACKED_KEYS` | `keyboard` | `32` — physical keys tracked at once (fixed slots) |
| `readGamepadActions(pad, state, buttons?, previousButtons?)` | `gamepad` | → held mask of one pad; updates stick hysteresis, `pressedButtons`, clears released `staleButtons` (a stale button gives `buttons[i] & previousButtons[i]`) |
| `GamepadLike`, `GamepadReadState` | `gamepad` | Pad fields read; per-pad `stickDirections`, optional `pressedButtons` / `staleButtons` bitmasks (indices 0–31); M3-01: `GamepadLike.vibrationActuator?` (`GamepadHapticLike` — its `playEffect?('dual-rumble', RumbleEffect)`) |
| `DEFAULT_GAMEPAD_BUTTONS`, `STICK_DEADZONE` (0.2), `STICK_HYSTERESIS` (0.1) | `gamepad` | Built-in standard-mapping button → actions table (fallback before a profile) and stick tuning |
| `rumblePad(pad, strength)`, `RUMBLE_EFFECTS`, `RumbleEffect`, `GamepadHapticLike` | `gamepad` | M3-01 (shmup_feat.md §4 "[P2] Rumble via `vibrationActuator.playEffect()`"): → `true` when an effect started (a pad without motors → `false`; a throw or a rejected promise is swallowed); strength 1 (a death) → 260 ms at 0.6 / 0.8, 2 (a boss blast) → 520 ms at 0.8 / 1.0 — frozen `{ startDelay, duration, weakMagnitude, strongMagnitude }` constants (index 0 unused) |
| `findKeyActions(code, keyCode, bindings)` | `keymap` | → mask, `0` (known, no action in this table) or `-1` (unknown key); `code` first, and a `code` entry of `0` falls through to the `keyCode` table |
| `resolveKeyActions(code, keyCode, bindings)` | `keymap` | → mask; unknown → `0` |
| `KeyBindings` | `keymap` | `{ byCode, byKeyCode }` → `ActionMask` |
| `DEFAULT_CODE_BINDINGS`, `DEFAULT_KEYCODE_BINDINGS`, `DEFAULT_KEY_BINDINGS` | `keymap` | Built-in fallback tables used before a profile is applied (one merged table, no contexts) |
| `TIZEN_KEY_CODES` | `keymap` | Remote key codes: arrows 37–40, Enter 13, Back 10009, MediaPlayPause 10252, Ch± 427/428, colours 403–406 |
| `createReleaseDebouncer(ticks, capacity = 32)` | `remote` | → `ReleaseDebouncer { ticks, capacity, setTicks, press(slot) → 'new' \| 'resumed' \| 'held', release(slot) → released now, isHeld, isReleasing, poll() → released count, reset, clear }`. A keyup between polls N and N+1 stays held through poll N+`ticks`, released on N+`ticks`+1; a keydown inside the window resumes without an edge. `ticks` clamped to 0…10, capacity floored, ≥ 1 (also for `NaN`); never allocates |
| `resolveDirections(mask, order, diagonals, socd)` | `remote` | → mask: SOCD per axis, then the diagonal policy (`order[bit]` = press order, higher = newer; ties: SOCD → neutral, `lastWins` → vertical, `firstWins` → horizontal). Pure, non-direction bits untouched |
| `createDirectionOrder()` | `remote` | → `DirectionOrder { order: Int32Array(4), update(mask), reset() }` — press order for polled devices (one per pad) |
| `InputTuning`, `DEFAULT_INPUT_TUNING` | `remote` | `{ releaseDebounceTicks, diagonals, socd, singleKey }`; default `0` / `'combine'` / `'neutral'` / `false`. `singleKey` (M3-02b): while any key is physically down the key source drops other keydowns — the Samsung remote delivers one key at a time |
