| `createWebStorage(backend \| null, { prefix?, quotaBytes?, valueMaxBytes?, onIssue? })` | `storage` | M2-17 — the web / TV hosts' one `localStorage` adapter → `QuotaStorage` (`PlatformStorage` + `usage()` + `issues`): keys under `prefix` (default `STORAGE_PREFIX`); a write over `valueMaxBytes` or making the app's keys exceed `quotaBytes` stays in memory for the session (`'over-budget'`); a browser quota error drops `DISPOSABLE_STORAGE_KEYS` and retries once, then keeps that value in memory with the backend still in use (`'quota-exceeded'`); any other error switches to memory for the session (`'unavailable'`); reads prefer a value kept in memory; never rejects; `onIssue(issue)` per issue |
| `QuotaStorage`, `WebStorageLike`, `WebStorageOptions` | `storage` | Above; `WebStorageLike` = `getItem`, `setItem`, optional `removeItem` / `key(i)` / `length` (`window.localStorage` is one) |
| `StorageIssue`, `StorageIssueKind`, `StorageUsage` | `storage` | `{ kind, key (unprefixed), bytes }`; `'over-budget' \| 'quota-exceeded' \| 'unavailable'`; `{ bytes, keys, quotaBytes, persistent }` (cold: scans the storage with `key()` / `length`, else the keys this session saw) |
| `storageBytes(key, value)`, `isQuotaExceededError(error)` | `storage` | → `(key.length + value.length) × 2` (UTF-16, as browsers count); → `true` for `QuotaExceededError`, `NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED`, codes 22 / 1014 |
| `STORAGE_PREFIX`, `STORAGE_QUOTA_BYTES`, `STORAGE_VALUE_MAX_BYTES`, `DISPOSABLE_STORAGE_KEYS` | `storage` | `'shmup-cup:'`; 1 MiB (all app keys); 256 KiB (one value, key included); `['save.corrupt']` — the replay library (M3-01, `core/replay` `MAX_REPLAY_TEXT` / `MAX_KEPT_REPLAY_TEXT`) takes at most 500,150 bytes of the 1 MiB, so the save and its corrupt copy still fit at 256 KiB each |
| `exportSaveText(store)`, `importSaveText(store, text)`, `SaveImportResult` | `storage` | M2-17 debug save export / import: → the document's canonical JSON pretty-printed; → `Promise<{ ok, status, reason, written }>` — `parseSave` (migrations, sanitising), `'empty'` / `'corrupt'` / `'unreadable'` refused (nothing changes), else `store.replace(data)` + `flush()`; never rejects |
| `estimateMemory({ atlasPages, keepPageImages?, sfxBytes, musicBytes, display?, frame?, filterTargets?, crtFilter?, crtAsFilter?, heapBytes?, budget? })`, `MemoryInputs`, `MemoryEstimate` | `memory` | M2-17, corrected by M3-02d (review **F3**) → frozen `{ textures, images, sfx, music, targets, heap, total, budget, withinBudget }`: pages `w × h × 4` (+ the decoded images unless `keepPageImages: false`), the targets (the frame target at its exact size — it is created directly — plus `filterTargets` pooled filter passes **rounded up to a power of two on each axis** (`potBytes`), plus the CRT's display-sized target when `crtAsFilter` and the setting is not `off` — **deliberately conservative**: it ignores `crtResolution`'s cap, so a 4K display is charged 4× the capped pass, and charges the whole display for a pillarboxed picture; the shipped blit path is charged **nothing** at any setting —, plus the display × 4 B × 3 canvas buffers, default 1920×1080), the heap baseline; `withinBudget` = total ≤ budget, atlas ≤ `TEXTURE_BUDGET_BYTES`, audio ≤ `AUDIO_BUDGET_BYTES` |
| `potBytes(w, h)`, `FILTER_TARGETS` | `memory` | M3-02d → `nextPow2(w) × nextPow2(h) × 4` (0 for a non-positive axis) — what Pixi's `TexturePool` really allocates for a pooled render target: a 384×216 filter pass is 512×256 = 512 KiB, a 1920×1080 one 2048×2048 = 16 MiB; `2` — the filter passes that can be live at once at the internal size |
| `estimateStageMemory({ content, manifest, sfx, music, stageIndex, needs?, display?, frame?, crtFilter?, crtAsFilter?, sampleRate? })`, `StageMemoryInputs` | `memory` | → the estimate of one stage: its pages (`stagePages`), the SFX bank (`sfxBankBytes`), its music set (`stageMusicTracks` → `trackBytes`); every campaign zone is tested under 100 MB |
| `pageBytes(page)`, `sfxBankBytes(sfx, rate?)`, `songFrameBound(song, rate?)`, `trackBytes(track, rate?)`, `stageMusicTracks(music, stage)` | `memory` | → `w × h × 4`; synthesized cues at the synth rate + 2 s of decoded stereo per recorded cue; a chip song's rendered frames without rendering it (rows × row samples, + `MAX_SONG_TAIL_SECONDS` for a one-shot); a track's bytes (chip: frames × 4; recorded: stereo 32 kHz to its loop end, else `FILE_TRACK_FALLBACK_SECONDS`); the title theme's track + every cue of `stageMusicCues(stage)`, resolved for the stage, each once |
| `stageSpriteSets(content)`, `StageSpriteSets` | `memory` | Load time → `{ byStage: (Set \| null)[], global: Set }` — each campaign zone's sprite ids (a walk of its stage through enemy / boss / child refs, tilesets and linked stages, every `sprite…Id`), `null` outside the campaign; sprites no zone names are global |
| `atlasPageNeeds(manifest, spriteNames, sets)`, `AtlasPageNeeds`, `stagePages(needs, stageIndex)` | `memory` | → `{ global[], stages[], campaign[] }` per page (a content sprite covers its `name@…` siblings; engine / UI sprites, font glyphs and loose frames make a page global); → the page indices a stage needs (every page for −1 or a stage outside the campaign) |
| `createAtlasResidency(pages, needs)`, `AtlasResidency`, `AtlasPageLike` | `memory` | → `{ resident[], unloads, prepare(stageIndex) → pages unloaded }` — `prepare` `unload()`s every resident page the stage does not need (Pixi re-uploads on the next draw); throws `RangeError` when the page counts differ; `AtlasPageLike` = `{ unload() }` (Pixi's `TextureSource`) |
| `connectAtlasResidency(dispatcher, residency)` | `memory` | Registers `PrepareStage` → `residency.prepare(event.id)` → an unregister function (`bootShell` does it in the scene flow) |
| `MEMORY_BUDGET_BYTES`, `TEXTURE_BUDGET_BYTES`, `AUDIO_BUDGET_BYTES`, `HEAP_BASELINE_BYTES`, `FILE_TRACK_FALLBACK_SECONDS`, `MIB` | `memory` | 100 MiB, 32 MiB, 32 MiB, 24 MiB (an assumption the §8.5 on-device check confirms), `180`, `1048576` |
| `createDeterminismCheck(contentFiles, now?)`, `DeterminismCheck` | `determinism` | M2-18 → `{ issues, play(replay) }`: validates the content like the boot (`loadGameContent` — the engine's scripts and sprites, the behaviour checks, the foreign owners); `play` decodes a `core/replay` document (`decodeReplay`; extra fields such as a golden file's `expected` ignored), builds a fresh session from its header (`createReplayGame` on `createHeadlessPlatform()`, input from `createPlayback` — god mode from `assisted`, the checkpoint), plays every recorded tick with the events dropped and → a `DeterminismRun`. `play` throws `Error` when the content had issues, `RangeError` for a document that is not a replay or names a stage / checkpoint the content lacks. Cold path — allocates the hash list and a session per call; one check plays any number of replays independently |
| `DeterminismRun` | `determinism` | `{ ok, checked, desyncTick (−1 = none), ticks, hashes, finalHash, status, ms }` — `hashes` are `hashWorld` in the page's engine after every `hashInterval`-th tick (the recording's ticks), `ok` / `checked` / `desyncTick` the playback's `DesyncReport`, `ms` from the injected clock (0 without one) |
| `installDeterminismCheck(win, contentFiles, now?)`, `DeterminismWindowLike` | `determinism` | M2-18 → the check, also published as `win[DETERMINISM_GLOBAL]` (`window.__shmupDeterminism`) with the root element's `DETERMINISM_READY_ATTRIBUTE` (`data-shmup-determinism`) set to `ready` — or `error` when the content has issues (the check still exists and lists them). `apps/web` `main.ts` calls it for `?determinism` in dev / test builds only; `test/e2e/determinism.spec.ts` waits for the attribute, then calls `play` with every golden replay and attract demo |
| `DETERMINISM_GLOBAL`, `DETERMINISM_READY_ATTRIBUTE` | `determinism` | `'__shmupDeterminism'`, `'data-shmup-determinism'` |

## Apps

These are not libraries, but their modules export testable functions.

### `apps/web`

| Export | Module | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `bootWebApp(canvas, resources, win?)` | `boot` | Runs the shell's `?scene=` (default `game`, the scene flow — M1-16) → `Promise<WebApp>` (`game`, `renderer`, `audio`, `input`, `profiles` (`InputProfileRegistry`), `shell`, `stop()`); `resources` = `WebAppResources { contentFiles, assets, debugTools? }` from the virtual modules (`debugTools`, M1-19: `main.ts` passes `debugToolsFactory({ buildId: __SHMUP_BUILD__ })` when `__SHMUP_DEV__`, else `null`). Key profile: `?profile=` › the saved choice (`options.input.profileId` of the save the shell reads before the title — only a selectable profile, M1-17) › `keyboard-default`; pads `gamepad-standard`; an unknown `?profile=` → `console.warn`. Passes `inputProfiles` to the shell: CONTROLS offers `KEYBOARD (DEFAULT)` / `KEYBOARD AS REMOTE` plus a `?profile=` override in use; a pick switches at once (with `?debounce=`), and a `?profile=` override wins over the saved choice, not over a pick. Since M2-16 the key and gamepad profiles are kept as written and applied with the player's rebinding, SOCD and debounce (`customizeInputProfile`; `?debounce=` applied after them); `inputProfiles` also has `customize` / `rebindable` (the key profile in use and `gamepad-standard`), so the rebind screen works. `?stage=<id>` → `gameConfig.stage`, else in the scene flow `defaultStageId(contentFiles)` — zone A (M1-18; the dev scenes such as `?scene=flight` keep open space); `?skip=` → `gameConfig.stageSkip` (M1-18, default `'none'`); `?loadout=` → `gameConfig.loadout` (M1-10 — with the MANTA of the ship select, M2-05, `'full'` is `applyDirectLoadout`'s: both levels 8 and the Hyper Arm); `remoteMode: false` (autofire stays on). Since M2-17, inside the Electron app (`getElectronBridge(win)` non-null) the platform is `'electron'` (file saves through the bridge, `exit` quits — EXIT on the title) and `audioUnlock` is `'immediate'`. Rejects with `ShellBootError`. M3-01: passes `resources.buildId` and SHARE (`copyReplayText`) to the shell and listens for pasted replays (`listenForPastedReplays` → `shell.replays.importText`; `stop()` removes it) |
| `copyReplayText(win, text)`, `listenForPastedReplays(win, importText)` | `boot` | M3-01: → `true` when `navigator.clipboard.writeText` was started (a secure context; a refusal is ignored), `false` without a clipboard; → a remover of a `paste` listener that imports text containing `"run-replay"` (other pastes are left alone) |
| `WebAppResources.buildId` | `boot` | M3-01: the build id the replays record (`main.ts` passes `__SHMUP_BUILD__`; default `'dev'`) |
| `inputOverridesFromSearch(search)` | `boot` | → `InputOverrides { profile: string \| null, debounce: number \| null }` from `?profile=<id>` / `?debounce=<0…10>`; percent-decoded, last valid value wins |
| `stageFromSearch(search)` | `boot` | → the `?stage=<id>` value (percent-decoded, last non-empty wins; malformed escapes ignored) or `null` |
| `loadoutFromSearch(search)` | `boot` | → `'full'` / `'default'` from `?loadout=<preset>` (exact, case-sensitive; last valid value wins) or `null` — the M1-10 dev override, applied as `GameConfig.loadout` (`'full'` includes a Force Field since M1-11 — for a Direct-mode MANTA both levels 8 and the gold Hyper Arm, M2-05) |
| `stageSkipFromSearch(search)` | `boot` | → `'boss'` / `'none'` from `?skip=<value>` (exact, case-sensitive, not percent-decoded; last valid value wins) or `null` — the M1-18 debug stage skip, applied as `GameConfig.stageSkip` (the e2e smoke reaches HALCYON BULWARK with it) |
| `determinismFromSearch(search)` | `boot` | M2-18 → `true` when the query has a `determinism` parameter (`?determinism`, any value or none; exact name, not percent-decoded). `main.ts` evaluates `__SHMUP_DEV__ && determinismFromSearch(location.search)`: when true it installs `@shmup/shell`'s `installDeterminismCheck` **instead of** calling `bootWebApp` (no WebGL, audio or input); a release build folds the branch away (`web-build.test.ts` checks the bundle has no `__shmupDeterminism`) |
| `contentStageIds(files)` | `boot` | → ids of every `stage` file among the raw content files (before validation); `bootWebApp` checks `?stage=` against it — an unknown id → `console.warn`, open space |
| `createWebPlatform(options)` | `platform` | → `Platform` (`id: 'web'`, `exit: null`); with `options.electron` (M2-17) `id: 'electron'`, storage `createBridgeStorage(electron)`, `exit` → `electron.quit()` |
| `createLocalStorage(storage \| null, prefix = STORAGE_PREFIX)` | `platform` | → `QuotaStorage` — the shell's `createWebStorage` with issues logged by `console.warn` (M2-17; before: the first error → memory for the session) |
| `getElectronBridge(win)`, `ElectronBridge` | `platform` | M2-17 → `window.shmupElectron` when it has the preload's shape (`platform: 'electron'`, `quit`, `storage.get` / `set`), else `null`; the type mirrors `apps/electron` `ShmupElectronApi` (a test keeps them in step). `bootWebApp` passes it to the platform and sets `audioUnlock: 'immediate'` with it |
| `createBridgeStorage(bridge)` | `platform` | M2-17 → `PlatformStorage` over the bridge (no prefix): a failing read resolves from an in-memory copy, a failing write rejects (the save store retries) |
| `createVisibilityLifecycle(source, focus)` | `platform` | → `PlatformLifecycle` from `visibilitychange` **and** the window's `blur` / `focus` (M3-02b; `focus: null` = visibility only). **Edge-triggered:** hidden and unfocused are two independent reasons to be away, the suspend callbacks run once on the edge into "away" (a `blur` + `hidden` pair suspends once) and the resume callbacks only when the page is visible *and* focused again; a repeated event with the same state fires nothing (the pre-M3-02b "repeated events fire the callbacks again" contract is gone) |
| `WebPlatformOptions`, `StorageLike`, `VisibilitySource`, `FocusSource` | `platform` | Injected browser services (`electron?` since M2-17; `StorageLike` = the shell's `WebStorageLike`; `focus?` since M3-02b — `bootWebApp` passes `window`, and Electron gets the same policy: a window that loses focus pauses) |

The frame loop moved to `@shmup/shell` (`startFrameLoop`).

### `apps/tizen`

| Export | Module | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `bootTizenApp(canvas, resources, win?)` | `boot` | → `Promise<TizenApp>` (`game`, `platform`, `renderer`, `audio`, `input`, `profiles` (`InputProfileRegistry`), `shell`, `stop()`); `resources` = `TizenAppResources { contentFiles, assets, debugTools? }` (`debugTools`, M1-19: `main.ts` passes `tizenDebugTools(window, __SHMUP_BUILD__, canvas)` when `__SHMUP_DEV__`, else `null`). Key profile: `tizen-remote-safe` in the platform factory, then the saved choice from the save the shell reads before the title (only a selectable profile; its keys registered — M1-17); pads `gamepad-standard`. `gameConfig`: `remoteMode` / `autofire` true and, in the scene flow, `stage: defaultStageId(contentFiles)` — START plays zone A (M1-18; the dev scenes keep open space, and there are no `?stage=` / `?skip=` parameters). Passes `inputProfiles` to the shell: CONTROLS offers `REMOTE (DEFAULT)` (M3-02b: the one remote profile — `tizen-remote-diagonal` was retired), and a pick switches at once and registers the new profile's `register` keys (since M3-02b also `Guide` and `Extra`). Since M2-16 the remote and gamepad profiles are applied with the player's rebinding, SOCD and debounce (a `ProfileState` keeps them as written — `customizeInputProfile`), and `inputProfiles` has `customize` / `rebindable` (the remote profile in use and `gamepad-standard`). The Back watcher is installed before boot and **removed once the shell runs** (M1-16): Back exits only from the loading and boot error screens; afterwards the scene flow owns Back (title → exit confirmation → `platform.exit()` after YES). M3-01: `resources.buildId` (`main.ts` passes `__SHMUP_BUILD__`) is the build id the replays record; the TV passes no SHARE hook (no clipboard) |
| `tizenDebugTools(win, buildId, canvas = null)` | `boot` | → the shell's `DebugToolsFactory` in `'sequence'` mode (M1-19): Pause, Ch+, Ch+, Ch+ unlocks the tools and its `onUnlock` registers `DEBUG_REMOTE_KEYS` with `tvinputdevice` once (nothing outside a TV); since M2-17 the unlock also collects `device-info` (the canvas's WebGL context for `MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE`, then `loadWebapis`) into the overlay's device line (`DebugToolsOptions.device`) and logs `Shmup Cup device` |
| `DEBUG_REMOTE_KEYS` | `boot` | `'1'` … `'8'` (frozen) — the remote's number keys, matching the shell's `digitKeyCode`s |
| `WEBGL_VERSION_KEY` | `boot` | M3-02c (review F8): `'shmup-cup:gl'`, the `localStorage` key a **dev / test** build reads for the WebGL version (`'2'` → 2, anything else → 1) — the TV has no query string, so this is its half of `webGLVersionFromSearch`'s `?gl=`. Read only where the debug tools exist (`__SHMUP_DEV__`), so a release bundle never looks at it; WebGL1 stays the shipped default and `PixiRenderer.webGLVersion` (the overlay's `WEBGL` figure) reports what was really obtained |
| `createTizenPlatform(options)` | `platform` | → `Platform` (`id: 'tizen'`, `remoteOnly: true`); registers `options.registerKeys` (the key profile's `register` list), else `REMOTE_KEYS_TO_REGISTER`. Its lifecycle takes `options.focus` (M3-02b — `bootTizenApp` passes `window`) beside `options.visibility` and is **edge-triggered** over hidden ∨ unfocused: Home on the M7 is an overlay that fires only `blur` / `focus`, so it suspends and resumes the game like being hidden, and a `blur` + `hidden` pair suspends once |
| `registerRemoteKeys(tizen, requested)` | `platform` | Batch registration with per-key fallback → names registered; drops `SYSTEM_REMOTE_KEYS` (`Exit`, volume) whatever the list says; an empty list registers nothing |
| `watchBackKey(target, onBack)` | `platform` | Calls `onBack` on non-repeat keyCode 10009 → unsubscribe function |
| `getTizenApi(win)` | `platform` | → `window.tizen` or `null` |
| `TizenApi`, `TizenPlatformOptions` (+ `registerKeys?`, `focus?` — M3-02b), `StorageLike`, `VisibilitySource`, `FocusSource` | `platform` | Types (`StorageLike` = the shell's `WebStorageLike` since M2-17 — the platform's storage is `createWebStorage` with issues logged) |
| `collectDeviceInfo(sources)`, `DeviceInfo`, `DeviceInfoSources` | `device-info` | M2-17 (implemented): pure → frozen `{ userAgent, chromeMajor, cssWidth, cssHeight, devicePixelRatio, webglVersion (1 \| 2 \| null), maxTextureSize, model, modelCode, firmware }` from `{ userAgent, innerWidth, innerHeight, devicePixelRatio, webglVersion, gl, webapis }` |
| `readProductInfo(webapis \| null)`, `ProductInfo`, `ProductInfoLike`, `WebapisLike` | `device-info` | → `{ model (getRealModel › getModel), modelCode, firmware }` — a missing getter, a throw (`SecurityError`) or an empty answer → `null`; never throws |
| `readMaxTextureSize(gl \| null)`, `GlParameterSource`, `parseChromeMajor(userAgent)` | `device-info` | → a positive whole `MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE` or `null`; → the `Chrome/NN` (or `Chromium/NN`) major or `null` |
| `formatDeviceLine(info)` | `device-info` | → `<model>[ <code>] FW <firmware> <w>x<h>@<dpr> C<chrome> GL<version>[/<max>]` (`?` / `-` for unknowns) — the overlay's line |
| `loadWebapis(win, timeoutMs = WEBAPIS_TIMEOUT_MS)`, `WEBAPIS_SCRIPT_URL`, `WEBAPIS_TIMEOUT_MS` | `device-info` | → `Promise<WebapisLike \| null>`: `window.webapis` at once when present, `null` without `window.tizen` (nothing requested), else one `<script src="$WEBAPIS/webapis/webapis.js">` per window → resolves on load / error / timeout; never rejects; `'$WEBAPIS/webapis/webapis.js'`, `3000` |
| `connectLiveReload({ url }, host)`, `LiveReloadOptions`, `LiveReloadHost`, `LiveReloadConnection`, `WebSocketLike` | `live-reload` | M2-17 (implemented, dev builds only — `main.ts` under `__SHMUP_DEV__` with a non-empty `__SHMUP_LIVE_RELOAD__`): → `{ attempts, stopped, stop() }`; opens a WebSocket (a throwing constructor counts as closed), on a `reload` message reloads in place or `location.replace`s the served page (`reloadTarget`), reconnects after `LIVE_RELOAD_RETRY_MS` doubling to `LIVE_RELOAD_MAX_RETRY_MS` (a message resets it); `host` = the `WebSocket` constructor, `location`, the timers (a `Window` is one) |
| `parseLiveReloadMessage(data)`, `LiveReloadMessage`, `reloadTarget(current, message)` | `live-reload` | → `{ type: 'reload' \| 'hello', url (http(s) or null) }` from JSON or the bare text `reload`, else `null`; → `null` to reload in place (no URL, or the same origin) or the page to open |
| `LIVE_RELOAD_RETRY_MS`, `LIVE_RELOAD_MAX_RETRY_MS` | `live-reload` | `1000`, `10000` |
| `REMOTE_KEYS_TO_REGISTER`, `TIZEN_BACK_KEY_CODE` | `platform` | Fallback when no input profile gives a `register` list: `MediaPlayPause`, `ChannelUp/Down`, `ColorF0Red…ColorF3Blue`; `10009` |
| `checkTizenBundle(distDir)` | `scripts/check-bundle.mjs` | → `{ problems, files, code, gzipBytes, distBytes }`: one script `app.js`, classic deferred tag, ES2018 parse, polyfill banner, widget files present, every other file under `dist/assets/`, at least one atlas page under `dist/assets/atlas/`, and the M1-19 budgets (below), and (M2-17) `config.xml` — whichever variant was built — passing `validateConfigXml`; `POLYFILL_BANNER`, `WIDGET_FILES` (`app.js`, `config.xml`, `icon.png`, `index.html`) |
| `APP_JS_GZIP_BUDGET`, `ATLAS_PAGE_MAX_SIZE`, `DIST_BUDGET`, `pngSize(bytes)` | `scripts/check-bundle.mjs` | `512 × 1024` bytes gzipped (since M3-02 — 350 KiB until M2-16, 384 KiB until M3-02; ≈ 375 KiB used at M3-01), `2048` px per page edge, `8 MiB` for `dist/` (M1-19); → `{ width, height }` from a PNG's IHDR, or `null` when not a PNG |
| `tizenCli()`, `sdbCli()`, `requireEnv()`, `resolveTarget()`, `run()`, `findWgt()`, `installableWgt()`, `removeWgts()`, `requireBuild()`, `APP_DIR`, `DIST_DIR`, `APP_ID` | `scripts/tizen-env.mjs` | Helpers of the Tizen CLI wrappers |

## Repo tooling

| Export | File | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `SOURCE_CONDITION` (`'@shmup/source'`), `clientConditions`, `serverConditions` | `vite.shared.ts` | Resolve workspace packages to `src/` in Vite/Vitest |
| `defineShmupProject(name, { environment?, include?, execArgv? })` | `vitest.shared.ts` | Per-project Vitest config (tests under `test/`, Node environment, `TEST_TIMEOUT_MS` 30 s); `execArgv` goes to the worker pool (`ALLOCATION_GUARD_EXEC_ARGV` in core, shell, render-pixi and input-web) |
| `ALLOCATION_GUARD_EXEC_ARGV` | `vitest.shared.ts` | `['--expose-gc', '--allow-natives-syntax']`: the worker flags `measureHeapGrowth` needs (`gc()`, V8's `%FinalizeOptimization()`) |
| `LongestFirstSequencer`, `MAX_WORKERS` | `vitest.config.ts` (root, not exported) | `pnpm test`'s one pool of `cores − 1` workers for every project (`VITEST_MAX_WORKERS` overrides); files start longest first across the projects (durations cached by Vitest), failed and new ones first |
| `measureHeapGrowth(fn, iterations, warmup = min(iterations, 1000), attempts = 3, settled = 32 KiB)` | `packages/core/test/helpers/alloc.ts` | The allocation guard of every package (plan §1.4) → `HeapGrowth { bytes, growth, collections, bytesPerIteration, windows }`: heap growth plus the bytes in-loop GCs reclaimed (V8 `GCProfiler`), without the `JIT_SPACES` (compiled code), the steadiest (fewest bytes) of up to `attempts` measured windows — the first window of at most `settled` bytes (keep it ≤ half the budget) ends the search. The warm-up (`WARMUP_ROUNDS` = 2 rounds, indices `0 … warmup − 1`) and the windows (the indices after it: `warmup + w × iterations …` in window `w`, so no index runs twice) run through one loop, each round after two full GCs and V8's `%FinalizeOptimization()`; throws without `ALLOCATION_GUARD_EXEC_ARGV`. Test-only, imported by relative path |
| `forceGc()`, `JIT_SPACES`, `WARMUP_ROUNDS` | `packages/core/test/helpers/alloc.ts` | Two full GCs for a retention check (`heapUsed` before and after a loop); the heap spaces the guard leaves out (`code_space`, `trusted_space` and their large-object and shared variants); the warm-up rounds |
| `shmupContent({ root? })` | `vite.shared.ts` | Vite plugin → `virtual:shmup-content`: every shipped `content/**/*.json` inlined into the bundle, sorted by path, `example.*.json` skipped, full reload on change |
| `readContentFiles(root?)`, `CONTENT_MODULE_ID`, `ContentFileRecord`, `ShmupContentOptions` | `vite.shared.ts` | The Node-side reader behind the plugin (also used by `pnpm content:check`): recursive, `example.*` skipped, sorted by path; `SyntaxError` naming the file on bad JSON, `[]` for a missing root |
| `virtual:shmup-content` | `types/virtual-modules.d.ts` | Ambient module declaration: `default: readonly { path, data }[]` |
| `shmupAssets({ sourceDir?, outDir? })` | `vite.shared.ts` | Vite plugin: runs the cached asset pipeline in `buildStart`, serves `virtual:shmup-assets`, emits the atlas pages into `dist/assets/atlas/` in builds; dev middleware for `<base>assets/atlas/*`, regenerate + full reload on `assets/source/` edits (pipeline-code edits: Vite restarts, or a warning). Throws `AssetSourceError` from the build hooks on invalid sources |

### Asset pipeline (`scripts/assets/`)

Plain Node ES modules with JSDoc types (Node-side TypeScript imports them through
`allowJs`). Guide: [asset-pipeline.md](asset-pipeline.md). Entry point:
`scripts/generate-assets.mjs` (`pnpm assets`; exports `parseArgs(argv)` →
`{ force, quiet, outDir?, sourceDir? }`, throws on unknown flags).

| Export | File | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `generateAssets({ sourceDir?, outDir?, force?, log? })` | `pipeline.mjs` | → `GenerateResult { cached, inputHash, manifest, atlasDir, files }`; writes `atlas/main*.png` + `atlas/main.json` + `.asset-cache.json` atomically, skips when the input hash and every output digest match, removes stale pages; throws `AssetSourceError` (nothing written) |
| `buildAtlas({ sourceDir?, maxPageSize? })` | `pipeline.mjs` | In memory → `{ manifest, pages: { file, image, png }[], sprites }`; throws `AssetSourceError` (invalid sources, a frame too large for a page) or `RangeError` (`maxPageSize` not a power of two) |
| `collectSprites({ sourceDir? })` | `pipeline.mjs` | → `{ sprites, fonts, issues }`: pixel maps + generators, PNG overrides, fonts, `@flash` siblings, default anchors, sorted by name |
| `computeInputHash(sourceDir?)` | `pipeline.mjs` | Hex SHA-256 of the pipeline code as loaded by this process (+ zlib, pngjs versions) and the current sprite/font sources |
| `AssetSourceError` | `pipeline.mjs` | `Error` with `issues: AssetIssue[]`; the message lists every issue |
| `pageFileName(i)`, `PIPELINE_DIR`, `REPO_ROOT`, `DEFAULT_SOURCE_DIR`, `DEFAULT_OUT_DIR`, `ATLAS_DIR` (`'atlas'`), `ATLAS_NAME` (`'main'`), `CACHE_FILE`, `ATLAS_PADDING` (1), `ATLAS_EXTRUDE` (1) | `pipeline.mjs` | Paths and layout constants; `pageFileName(0)` = `main.png`, `pageFileName(1)` = `main-1.png` |
| `AtlasManifest`, `ManifestPage`, `ManifestFrame`, `ManifestSprite` | `manifest.mjs` | JSDoc typedefs of the manifest (mirrored by `virtual:shmup-assets`) |
| `MANIFEST_FORMAT_VERSION` (1), `frameName(sprite, i)` (→ `<sprite>#<i>`), `formatManifest(m)` | `manifest.mjs` | Format version, frame naming, byte-stable serialiser (sorted keys, one entry per line) |
| `findMissingSprites(manifest, names, label = 'sprites')` | `manifest.mjs` | → `AssetIssue[]`, one per name not in `manifest.sprites` (`<label>[<i>]`); used by `pnpm content:check` |
| `parseSpriteSource(json, file, expectedName?)` | `sprite-source.mjs` | Validates one `*.sprite.json` → `{ sprite: SpriteDef \| null, issues }`; never throws on bad data |
| `loadSpriteSources(dir, displayRoot)` | `sprite-source.mjs` | → `{ sprites, overrides: PngSprite[], issues }` for a `sprites/` tree |
| `readPngFrames(image, sidecar, file, issues)`, `applyPngOverrides(sprites, overrides)` | `sprite-source.mjs` | Cut frames / tags / pivot out of a PNG + Aseprite export; merge overrides by name (frames by index, extras appended, inputs untouched) |
| `listFiles(dir)`, `SPRITE_NAME_PATTERN`, `ANIMATION_NAME_PATTERN`, `SPRITE_SOURCE_SUFFIX` | `sprite-source.mjs` | Sorted recursive listing (`/` separators, `[]` for a missing dir); name rules |
| `SpriteDef`, `PngSprite`, `AssetIssue` | `sprite-source.mjs` | Typedefs: `{ name, anchor, hitFlash, frames, animations, origin }`; `{ path, message }` (same shape as `ValidationIssue`) |
| `parseFontSource(json, file)`, `loadFontSources(dir, displayRoot)`, `buildFontSprite(font)` | `font.mjs` | Validate `*.font.json`; → `{ sprite: font/<name>, metrics: FontMetrics }` |
| `FontDef`, `FontMetrics`, `FONT_SOURCE_SUFFIX`, `FONT_NAME_PATTERN`, `ASCII_PRINTABLE` | `font.mjs` | Font typedefs and rules; `ASCII_PRINTABLE` = code points 32…126 |
| `packRects(items, { maxSize?, minSize?, padding?, extrude? })` | `packer.mjs` | Deterministic MaxRects → `PackResult { pages, placements }` (input order); throws `RangeError` on duplicates, bad sizes/options, an item too large for `maxSize²`. `MAX_PAGE_SIZE` = 2048; typedefs `PackItem`, `PackOptions`, `Placement`, `PackResult` |
| `encodePng(image)`, `decodePng(bytes)`, `crc32(bytes, crc?)`, `PNG_SIGNATURE` | `png.mjs` | Zero-dependency RGBA encoder (deterministic per zlib version; `RangeError` on a bad buffer); `pngjs` decoder for any PNG → RGBA |
| `createImage`, `parseColor`, `setPixel`, `getPixel`, `blit`, `crop`, `flipHorizontal`, `flipVertical`, `imageFromRows`, `imagesEqual`; `Image`, `Rgba` | `image.mjs` | Straight-alpha RGBA rasters `{ width, height, data }` |
| `whiteSilhouette(frame)`, `makeFlashSprite(sprite)`, `FLASH_SUFFIX` (`'@flash'`) | `flash.mjs` | D30 hit-flash sprites |
| `makeP2Sprite(sprite)`, `swapRedBlue(frame)`, `wantsP2Variant(name)`, `P2_SUFFIX` (`'@p2'`), `P2_VARIANT_PREFIXES` (`['ships/']`), `P2_VARIANT_SPRITES` (`['hud/life']`) | `coop.mjs` | M2-06: player 2's palette swap — `collectSprites` adds `<name>@p2` (red and blue channels swapped, same anchor / frames / animations, `hitFlash: false`) for every ship and the stock icon, after the `@flash` siblings |
| `createAssetRng(seed)` → `AssetRng { nextU32, nextFloat, rangeInt, chance }`, `hash2(x, y, seed)` | `rng.mjs` | sfc32 with its own splitmix32 seeding (sequences differ from `core/rng`); stateless position hash for tiling textures |
| `PROCEDURAL_GENERATORS`, `generateProceduralSprites()` | `procedural/index.mjs` | Registry `{ id, generate }[]`; every generator's sprites in registry order |
| `DIRECTIONS_8`, `color`, `mix`, `withAlpha`, `seedOf`, `makeSprite` | `procedural/common.mjs` | Exact 22.5° headings, colour helpers, FNV-1a name seed, `SpriteDef` builder (`origin: procedural:<id>`) |
| `fillEllipse(image, cx, cy, rx, ry, paint)`, `drawLine(image, x0, y0, x1, y1, rgba, width = 1)` | `procedural/common.mjs` | M2-11 shape helpers: `paint(u, v, e, x, y)` → an `Rgba` or `null` for every pixel whose centre lies inside the ellipse (`u` / `v` offsets in radii, `e` the normalised distance, clipped to the image); a straight line of round dots of diameter `width` (clipped) |
| `generate()` per module; `BULLET_COLORS`, `METER_LABELS`, `TERRAIN_TILES`, `TILE_SIZE` (8), `STAR_TILE_SIZE` (128) | `procedural/*.mjs` | The generators (M2-02: `palettes` — every bullet, beam and bending laser segment again as `<sprite>@<palette>` for `deuteranopia` / `protanopia` / `tritanopia`, recoloured with its `BULLET_PALETTES` body colours and shape-coded with `CORE_MARKS` (pink `solid`, red `ring`, purple `dot`); `bullets` gained `bulletSprites(colours, suffix?, marks?, generator?)` and the `CoreMark` typedef, `lasers` `laserSprites(…)`, the `lasers/bend-{pink,red,purple}` 7×7 segments and `BEND_SIZE`, `items` the 5×5 `items/point` diamond with a `twinkle` animation; `backdrops` — M1-18: `bg/azure-verge`, zone A's planet-rim parallax band, a seamless `AZURE_TILE_W` 128 × `AZURE_TILE_H` 48 tile, haze above the lit rim row `AZURE_RIM_ROW` 10, then an opaque azure-to-navy body with seeded cloud streaks that wrap — `bullets`, `explosions`, `hud`, `items`, `lasers` — M1-09: `lasers/beam-{pink,red,purple}`, 8 frames of 4×8, frame `k` a band `k + 1` px tall; `BEAM_WIDTH`, `BEAM_HEIGHT`, `bandRows` — `particles`, `shields`, `starfield`, `terrain`, `ui` — M1-16: `ui/logo`, 165×27, from original 5×7 block letters; `LOGO_TEXT` `'SHMUP CUP'`, `LOGO_SCALE` 3 — `weapons` — M2-03: `shots/blast` (32×32, 4 frames), `shots/ripple` (24×44, 6 frames), `shots/cyclone` (8×9, 4 frames); `BLAST_SIZE`, `RIPPLE_W`, `RIPPLE_H`, `RIPPLE_FRAMES`, `CYCLONE_W`, `CYCLONE_H` — and `hud`'s `METER_LABELS` grew to 16 frames, M2-03; M2-04: `items` the blue capsule `items/capsule-blue` (the capsule pill in blue — `capsule(bright, tint)`), `shields` the pod `shields/pod` (8×8, 4 wear frames) and Reduce's `shields/reduce` (20×14 dotted ring, 2 frames); M2-05: `direct` — the MANTA's shots `shots/direct-missile` (12×6, 2 frames), `shots/disc` (20×20, 4), `shots/beam` (26×8, 4), `shots/wave` (16×34, 4), `shots/sub-bomb`, `shots/sub-laser` / `sub-laser-wide` (8 octant frames each — never rotated at run time), `shots/sub-disc` (2), the six colour items `items/direct-<colour>` (10×10, 2 frames) and the Arm `shields/arm` (26×18, 9 frames: tier × fresh / worn / critical); M2-08: `raster-bands` — `bg/sea-swell` (`SEA_TILE_W` 128 × `SEA_TILE_H` 40, only the four `SEA_RAMP` colours — a palette cycle's ramp) and `bg/checker-floor` (`FLOOR_TILE_W` 64 × `FLOOR_TILE_H` 48, strips `FLOOR_BANDS` with checker widths `FLOOR_SQUARES`, `FLOOR_COLORS`) for the raster effects; M2-09: `bosses` — `bosses/turret` (16×16, 16 heading frames, the barrel at `k × 22.5°` clockwise from +x — a turned part's `turn: 16`), `bosses/orb` (12×12, 2 frames, `pulse`), `bosses/raid-hull` (96×40 riveted hull section, seeded rivets) and `bosses/captain-shell` (28×20, 2 frames, `blink`), each with its hit flash; M2-10: `items` gained `items/capsule-bonus` (the gold pill) and `items/1up` (the green pill with a white `+`), 12×8 × 2 frames, `blink`) and their data; M2-11: `brine` — zone B's art, `BRINE_SPRITES` in order (`bg/brine-nebula` `NEBULA_TILE_W` 128 × `NEBULA_TILE_H` 64, `bg/brine-sea` `BRINE_SEA_W` 128 × `BRINE_SEA_H` 48 painted only in `BRINE_RAMP` — the four colours zone B's palette cycle names —, the enemies `froth`, `froth-bead`, `brood-bubble`, `gill-dart`, `reef-jelly`, `urchin`, `maw-rocket`, GALVANIC MAW's `maw-hull`, `maw-jaw-top` / `-bottom`, `maw-core`, `maw-pod`, `maw-fin` and SPUME HERALD's `herald-shell`) —, `dune` — zone C's, `DUNE_SPRITES` (`bg/dune-suns` `SUNS_TILE_W` 72 × `SUNS_TILE_H` 40, `bg/dune-ridge` `RIDGE_TILE_W` 128 × `RIDGE_TILE_H` 40, the enemies `dune-worm`, `husk-crawler`, `sand-skimmer`, `dust-devil`, `sand-geyser`, `sand-clod`, `widow-drone`, SANDGRAVE WIDOW's `widow-body`, `widow-head`, `widow-fang`, `widow-leg-top` / `-bottom`, `widow-spinneret`) — and `terrain` draws one tileset per `TERRAIN_PALETTES` entry (`tiles/terrain-a`, `tiles/terrain-reef`, `tiles/terrain-dune`; typedef `TerrainPalette { surface, subsurface, rock }`) — guide: [zones-b-and-c.md](zones-b-and-c.md#placeholder-art-scriptsassetsprocedural); M2-12: `magma` — zone D's art, `MAGMA_SPRITES` (13, in order: `bg/magma-peaks` `PEAKS_TILE_W` 128 × `PEAKS_TILE_H` 56, `bg/magma-lava` `LAVA_TILE_W` 128 × `LAVA_TILE_H` 40 painted only in `MAGMA_RAMP` — the four colours zone D's palette cycle names —, the enemies `ember-wisp`, `cinder-bat`, `magma-cone`, `magma-bomb`, `cinder-rock`, `slag-crawler`, `basalt-turret`, CINDER BASTION's `bastion-hull`, `bastion-core`, `bastion-emitter`, `bastion-arm`) —, `tempest` — zone E's, `TEMPEST_SPRITES` (17: `bg/storm-clouds` `CLOUDS_TILE_W` 128 × `CLOUDS_TILE_H` 56 painted only in `STORM_RAMP`, `bg/storm-ridge` `RIDGE_TILE_W` 128 × `RIDGE_TILE_H` 48, `bg/storm-rain` `RAIN_TILE_W` 64 × `RAIN_TILE_H` 64 seamless both ways, the enemies `hail-drifter`, `gale-kite`, `squall-jumper`, `crag-turret`, `thunderhead`, `steed-foal`, SQUALL STEED's `steed-body`, `steed-head`, `steed-snout`, `steed-chest`, `steed-lid-top` / `-bottom`, `steed-tail`, `steed-fin`) — and `terrain`'s `TERRAIN_PALETTES` gained `tiles/terrain-magma` and `tiles/terrain-ridge` — guide: [zones-d-and-e.md](zones-d-and-e.md#placeholder-art-scriptsassetsprocedural); M2-13: `vault` — zone F's art, `VAULT_SPRITES` (14, in order: `bg/vault-membrane` `MEMBRANE_TILE_W` 128 × `MEMBRANE_TILE_H` 64 — a seeded cell wall wrapped both ways, painted only in `VAULT_RAMP`, the four colours zone F's palette cycle names —, `bg/vault-folds` `FOLDS_TILE_W` 128 × `FOLDS_TILE_H` 48, the enemies `lymph-mote`, `chaser-cell`, `mitosis-cell`, `vault-claw`, `polyp-turret`, `spore-sac`, MANTLE REGENT's `regent-mantle`, `regent-fin` (decoration, no hit flash), `regent-eye`, `regent-root`, `regent-segment`, `regent-tip`) —, `prism` — zone G's, `PRISM_SPRITES` (14: `bg/prism-facets` `FACETS_TILE_W` 128 × `FACETS_TILE_H` 64 — 16-px facet cells in the four `PRISM_RAMP` colours —, `bg/prism-spires` `SPIRES_TILE_W` 128 × `SPIRES_TILE_H` 56, the enemies `glint-mote`, `prism-cube`, `facet-turret`, `halo-crystal`, `prism-lens`, `geode`, `geode-shard`, FACET MONARCH's `facet-body` (decoration, no hit flash), `facet-core`, `facet-crystal`, `facet-segment`, `facet-tip`) — and `TERRAIN_PALETTES` gained `tiles/terrain-vault` and `tiles/terrain-prism` — guide: [zones-f-and-g.md](zones-f-and-g.md#placeholder-art-scriptsassetsprocedural); M2-14: `citadel` — zone H's art, `CITADEL_SPRITES` (14: `bg/citadel-wall` `WALL_TILE_W` 128 × `WALL_TILE_H` 64 — riveted panels, a running light per panel in one of the four `CITADEL_RAMP` colours zone H's palette cycle names —, `bg/citadel-pipes` `PIPES_TILE_W` 128 × `PIPES_TILE_H` 48, the enemies `bolt-drone`, `hatch-bay`, `hatch-mite`, `laser-emitter`, `sentinel-walker`, `rail-turret`, IRON SOVEREIGN's `sovereign-hull`, `-core`, `-plate`, `-pod`, `-emitter`, `-hatch`) —, `abyss` — zone I's, `ABYSS_SPRITES` (19: `bg/abyss-murk` `MURK_TILE_W` 128 × `MURK_TILE_H` 64 — specks in the four `ABYSS_RAMP` colours —, `bg/abyss-spires` `SPIRES_TILE_W` 128 × `SPIRES_TILE_H` 56, the enemies `lumen-mote`, `depth-mine`, `trench-eel`, `gulper`, `abyss-turret`, `ark-hook`, the ABYSS ARK's `ark-bow`, `ark-hull`, `ark-stern`, `ark-turret` (16 heading frames), `ark-heart`, THE HOLLOW KING's `king-body`, `king-jaw-top` / `-bottom`, `king-maw`, `king-stalk`, `king-lure`) —, `ending` — the ending scenes' UI sprites, `ENDING_SPRITES` (6: `ui/ending-citadel`, `ui/ending-ark`, `ui/ending-blast` (4 frames), `ui/ending-bubble`, `ui/ending-sun`, `ui/ending-surface` — `core/ui` `UI_SPRITES`) — and `TERRAIN_PALETTES` gained `tiles/terrain-citadel` and `tiles/terrain-abyss`; ### Playtest (`test/playtest/`)

The headless playtest of plan M1-18 (plan §1.4) — test code in the `integration` Vitest project,
imported by relative path. Guide: [zone-a-and-playtest.md](zone-a-and-playtest.md#the-playtest-testplaytest).

| Export | File | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `runStage(stageId, bot, flags = {})` | `harness.ts` | Plays a shipped stage in bare gameplay (`createGame` on the headless platform, the shipped content) with `bot` at player 1's controls until `stageClear`, `gameOver` or `maxTicks` → `PlaytestResult` (since M2-11 the boss figures follow the main encounter — the first slot with a boss of role `boss` in its fight or death —, never a captain); throws `Error` for content issues, `RangeError` for an unknown stage or a bad config |
| `PlaytestBot` | `harness.ts` | `{ name, decide(world) → Action mask, remoteStrict? }` — called once per tick before it runs; the harness commits the mask like an input adapter (presses are edges of the held mask). `remoteStrict` (M3-02b) opts the run into the model check |
| `PlaytestFlags` | `harness.ts` | `godMode?` (on `world.debugFlags`), `seed?` (1), `stageSkip?` (`'none'`), `config?` (other `GameConfig` fields — `seed`, `stage`, `stageSkip` win), `maxTicks?` (`DEFAULT_MAX_TICKS`), `observe?(world)` (after every tick; read only) |
| `PlaytestResult`, `PlaytestDeath` | `harness.ts` | `stageId`, `bot`, `godMode`, `seed`, `status`, `ticks`, `clearTick` (−1), `seconds`, `bossDefeated`, `bossFightTicks` (−1), `deaths` (`tick`, `cameraX`, `cause` — `PLAYER_HIT_CAUSE_NAMES` —, `y`, `boss`, `livesLeft`), `score`, `pickups`, `equips` (per `MeterSlot`), `diagonalTicks`, `shipX { min, max }`, `inputs` (`Uint16Array`, one held mask per tick), `hash` (`hashWorld`); M3-02b `remoteViolations` / `remoteViolation` (0 / `''` when not checked) |
| `replayStage(stageId, inputs, flags = {})`, `ReplayOutcome` | `harness.ts` | Replays a recording in a fresh session with the same flags → `{ status, ticks, deathTicks, hash }` (equal to the run's) |
| `describeRun(run)` | `harness.ts` | → one-line summary (`zone-a four-way (god mode): stageClear after 210.2 s, boss 21.7 s, 0 death(s), …`) |
| `shippedContent()` | `harness.ts` | → the shipped `ContentDb`, validated like the shell's boot (`KNOWN_SCRIPT_IDS`, `ENGINE_SPRITES`, the enemy / weapon behaviour checks), cached per worker; throws `Error` listing the issues |
| `TICKS_PER_SECOND`, `DEFAULT_MAX_TICKS`, `DIRECTIONS` | `harness.ts` | `60`; ten minutes of ticks (36,000); the four direction bits of `Action` |
| `fourWayBot(player = 0, options?)` | `four-way-bot.ts` | → a `PlaytestBot` named `four-way` flying that player slot (M2-06 — the co-op goldens fly player 2 with a second bot): never two directions, x ≈ `BOT_X`, up / down to the cheapest lane of the danger scan (trip + stay + terrain − preferences, 20-point hysteresis), then back to `BOT_X`; one-tick `PowerUp` presses on Speed (≤ `BOT_MAX_SPEED_LEVEL`), Missile, Option. Since M2-10 it faces the first boss slot in its intro or fight (it read slot 0 only before). **Since M3-02b it flies under the `remote-strict` model by default** (`{ remote: false }` = the old bot): it waits for a lane clear for `EQUIP_WINDOW_TICKS` (12) before an equip tap — `EQUIP_PATIENCE_TICKS` (150) is the "take the risk" deadline —, prefers a boss core's lane by `BOSS_CORE_BONUS` (160, was 60) and, **in god mode only**, sits on the core's exact row. One bot per run |
| `createRemoteStrictModel()`, `RemoteStrictModel` | `remote-strict.ts` | M3-02b → `{ filter(want) → mask, reset() }` — the mask a Samsung-remote player could really produce this tick: one key at a time, a button press is a direction-free tap of `REMOTE_TAP_TICKS` after `REMOTE_PRE_TAP_TICKS` empty ticks, a direction change costs `REMOTE_GAP_TICKS`, a release-only action lasts `REMOTE_EDGE_TICKS` |
| `createRemoteStrictCheck()`, `RemoteStrictCheck` | `remote-strict.ts` | M3-02b: reads a recorded run back tick by tick and counts every violation of the model (`PlaytestResult.remoteViolations` / `.remoteViolation`; a violation fails a run like `diagonalTicks`) |
| `REMOTE_DIRECTIONS`, `REMOTE_BUTTONS`, `REMOTE_EDGE_ACTIONS`, `REMOTE_EDGE_TICKS`, `REMOTE_TAP_MIN_TICKS`, `REMOTE_TAP_MAX_TICKS`, `REMOTE_TAP_TICKS`, `REMOTE_PRE_TAP_TICKS`, `REMOTE_GAP_TICKS` | `remote-strict.ts` | M3-02b, from the 2026-09-15 measurements: the four directions; PowerUp \| Special \| Speed; Pause; `1`; `7`; `16`; `10`; `2`; `1` |
| `scanLanes(world, scan, player = 0)`, `createLaneScan()`, `LaneScan` | `four-way-bot.ts` | Fills `LaneScan { centre, span: Uint32Array (slot bits per lane), terrain: Float64Array, wall: Uint8Array }` (cleared first) from bullets, bodies, boss parts, lasers (from 12 ticks before the beam grows) and rock within `TERRAIN_AHEAD` |
| `laneCentre(lane)`, `laneOf(y)` | `four-way-bot.ts` | A lane's centre row; the lane of a playfield row (clamped) |
| `BOT_X`, `LANE_HEIGHT`, `LANES`, `BULLET_HORIZON`, `SLOT_TICKS`, `SLOTS`, `TERRAIN_AHEAD`, `BOT_MAX_SPEED_LEVEL` | `four-way-bot.ts` | `64`, `16`, `12`, `40`, `2`, `20`, `56`, `2` |
| `maxBulletSpeed(world)` | `rules.ts` | → the fastest live enemy bullet (px/tick; 0 without) |
| `laserLaneGaps(world)`, `LaneGaps` | `rules.ts` | → `{ lanes, separate, narrowestBetween (Infinity with < 2), widestOpen }` — lanes = lasers in telegraph / grow / active, rows widened by the hurt radius and clipped to the playfield (a beam wholly off it is no lane), overlapping lanes merged |
| `createRuleWatch()`, `RuleWatch` | `rules.ts` | A collector: `observe(world)` (bound — pass it as `PlaytestFlags.observe`), `maxBulletSpeed`, `maxLanes`, `maxSeparate`, `narrowestGap`, `narrowestOpen`, since M2-18 `narrowestColumn` (the narrowest `columnGap`; below `MIN_LANE_GAP` a violation), `violations` (the first 20, `tick N: …`) |
| `columnGap(world, player = 0)` | `rules.ts` | M2-18 → the widest open band of playfield rows (px) in the ship's column: every live enemy bullet within `COLUMN_HALF_WIDTH` + its radius of the ship's x closes its rows ± its radius and the hurt radius, every live laser lane (not fading) its rows like `laserLaneGaps`; bands clipped to the playfield and merged; `PLAYFIELD_H` when nothing crosses. Test code — allocates |
| `MAX_AIMED_BULLET_SPEED`, `MIN_LANE_GAP`, `COLUMN_HALF_WIDTH` | `rules.ts` | `2.0` px/tick (D17, Normal); `16` px (lanes and, since M2-18, the ship's column); `8` px either side of the ship (M2-18) |
| `playRunZone(run, bot, flags = {})` | `campaign.ts` | M2-10: plays the run's current zone as the scene flow builds it (`runWorldConfig` + `prepareRunWorld`, the players carried from `run.entry`) until `stageClear` / `gameOver` / `maxTicks`, then tallies, pays and carries out (`tallyZone`, `awardZoneBonus`, `captureCarry`) → `{ outcome: ZoneOutcome, world }` |
| `walkCampaignRoutes(campaign, newBot, flags = {}, start?)` | `campaign.ts` | M2-10: walks the route tree depth-first — each zone played once per distinct route prefix (31 zone runs for the 16 routes of the diamond), the run state copied at every fork (`cloneRun`); `start` limits it to the routes through some of the start zone's exits → one `RouteOutcome` per route, top route first |
| `cloneRun(run)`, `CampaignFlags`, `ZoneOutcome`, `RouteOutcome` | `campaign.ts` | M2-10: an independent copy of a `RunState`; `{ godMode? (default true), seed?, config?, maxTicks?, observe?(world) }` (`observe` — M2-18 — after every tick of every zone, read only; the route runs pass `createRuleWatch().observe`); `{ zone, stageId, status, ticks, killPercent, timeBonus, score, rankStage }`; `{ route, labels (`ABDFH`), zones, cleared, ending, score }` |
| `shippedStage(stageId)` | `recovery.ts` | M2-11: a shipped `StageSpec`; throws `Error` for an unknown id |
| `capsuleSources(stage)` | `recovery.ts` | M2-11: the x of every capsule source — a `spawn` of an enemy whose `drop` is `capsule`, a `formation` whose `drop` is not `null` |
| `perfectFrom(stageId, checkpoint, until)` | `recovery.ts` | M2-11: restarts the stage at a checkpoint (god mode, no autofire) and kills every enemy on its first on-screen tick until the camera reaches `until` → `{ drops, cameraX }` |
| `recoveryAt(stageId, checkpoint)` | `recovery.ts` | M2-11: the recovery check of one checkpoint — the sources in its 900-px window and a perfect player's drops until the next source beyond it (or the WARNING) → `{ inWindow, drops, cameraX, until }`; used by `zone-a-recovery`, `zone-bc-recovery`, (M2-12) `zone-de-recovery` — zone D's third checkpoint lies in the caves, the restart puts the camera back down there — (M2-13) `zone-fg-recovery` and (M2-14) `zone-hi-recovery` |
| `RECOVERY_WINDOW`, `MIN_RECOVERY_CAPSULES` | `recovery.ts` | `900` px; `3` (shmup_feat.md §10) |
| `flyRoutesThrough(exitLabel, ship = 'kestrel')` | `campaign-routes.ts` | M2-10: the shared checks of `campaign-routes-b` / `-c.test.ts` (since M2-18 also `-manta-b` / `-manta-c`, the `ship` argument) — flies every route through that exit of zone A with `fourWayBot()` in god mode and asserts each zone's clear, each zone's length in `ZONE_MIN_SECONDS`–`ZONE_MAX_SECONDS` and the 4-way rules on every tick (M2-18), the rank stage term, the growing score and an ending of the final zone — since M2-14 one with its sprite scene and its epilogue (the real finales H and I) → the routes |
| `ZONE_MIN_SECONDS`, `ZONE_MAX_SECONDS`, `ROUTE_SHIPS`, `RouteShip` | `campaign-routes.ts` | M2-18: `180`, `360` (a zone lasts 3–6 min, shmup_feat.md §14); frozen `{ kestrel: {}, manta: { shipId: 'manta', powerUpMode: 'direct' } }` — the `CampaignFlags.config` of each ship; `'kestrel' \| 'manta'` |

### Golden replays (`test/golden/`)

Test code of plan M1-19 in the `integration` Vitest project, imported by relative path. Guide:
[debug-and-replays.md](debug-and-replays.md#golden-replays-testgolden).

| Export | File | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `GOLDEN_SCENARIOS`, `GoldenScenario` | `golden.ts` | The sixty-two committed runs — since M3-01 six of the extra modes (`zone-a-loop2-god` / `zone-a-loop2-boss` — `loop: 2`, the remix, faster and revenge bullets; `zone-a-caravan` — `timeLimit` 3600, time up; `zone-a-extra` — Hawk Wind and the Spread Gun; `zone-a-recovery` — `optionRecovery`, the weaver; `zone-a-invincible` — `invincible`, the weaver, `assists` 2),  since M2-16 two of the autofire modes (`remoteMode: false`, god mode, the full loadout, the stage skip to HALCYON BULWARK: `zone-a-manta-toggle` — the MANTA in the `'toggle'` mode, `Shot` tapped every `TOGGLE_TAP_TICKS`; `zone-a-hold` — the KESTREL in the `'hold'` mode at `autofireInterval` 2, `Shot` / `Sub` held in bursts), since M2-14 seven of the final zones H and I (`zone-h-god`, `zone-i-god`: the 4-way bot with god mode, start to stage clear — the parade, IRON SOVEREIGN; the ARK raid and THE HOLLOW KING; `zone-h-boss`, `zone-i-boss`: the stage skips — zone H's lands before the parade —, full loadout, Arcade penalty; `zone-h-arcade`: the 4-way bot at Arcade difficulty without god mode, the clear; `zone-h-deaths`: the weaver on Easy under the Arcade penalty to game over; `zone-i-escape`: the weaver with god mode and no power-ups, the stage skip — the ARK escapes after its 90-s time limit, `EndingFlag.BossEscaped`, no king), since M2-13 eight of the real zones F and G (`zone-f-god`, `zone-g-god`: the 4-way bot with god mode, start to stage clear; `glimmer-cache-god`: zone G's bonus stage with the full loadout; `zone-f-arcade`: the 4-way bot at Arcade difficulty without god mode, the clear; `zone-f-deaths`: the weaver on Easy under the Arcade penalty to game over, the restarts rolling the tissue back; `zone-g-bot`: the 4-way bot without god mode, a death in the cube rush and the clear; `zone-f-boss`, `zone-g-boss`: the stage skips, full loadout, Arcade penalty), since M2-12 five of the real zones D and E (`zone-d-god`, `zone-e-god`: the 4-way bot with god mode, start to stage clear; `zone-d-bot`, `zone-e-bot`: the 4-way bot without god mode, a death and the clear — zone D's down in the caves; `zone-d-boss`: the stage skip into the caves, full loadout, Arcade penalty), since M2-11 five of the real zones B and C (`zone-b-god`, `zone-c-god`: the 4-way bot with god mode, start to stage clear; `brine-grotto-god`: zone B's bonus stage with the full loadout; `zone-b-deaths`: the weaver under the Arcade penalty to game over; `zone-c-bot`: the 4-way bot without god mode, a death and the clear), since M2-10 three of the bonus-stage dev stages (`bonus-range-god`: the full loadout, the `ground` entrance opens; `bonus-range-digit`: no power-ups, the `digit` entrance on the thousands digit 0; `bonus-vault-god`: the bonus stage — bonus capsules and the 1UP), since M2-09 four of the advanced-boss dev stages (`captain-range-god`: the 4-way bot with god mode against the four captains; `raid-range-god`: the full loadout against IRON LEVIATHAN and LEVIATHAN HEART; `raid-range-escape`: no power-ups — the battleship escapes after its time limit, `EndingFlag.BossEscaped`; `twin-range-god`: the full loadout against the twins — turns, enrage), since M2-08 one of the dev stage `raster-range` (`raster-range-god`: the 4-way bot with god mode; also played back with the stage's `raster` / `cycles` stripped — every hash matches), since M2-07 three of the dev stage `gimmick-range` (`gimmick-range-god`: the 4-way bot with god mode — the high branch, a brick, blocks, suction, a tentacle, the cube rush; `gimmick-range-weaver`: the weaver with god mode through the region trigger — the low branch; `gimmick-range-deaths`: the weaver under the Arcade penalty — terrain rollbacks, game over) besides the zone A runs (`zone-a-god`, `zone-a-arcade`, `zone-a-deaths`, `zone-a-boss`, since M2-03 the arsenal against the boss: `zone-a-type-b`, `zone-a-edit`, `zone-a-type-c`, `zone-a-type-d`, since M2-04 the Option types and meter shields: `zone-a-rotate`, `zone-a-reduce` (the boss), `zone-a-snake`, `zone-a-free-shield` (the whole stage), and since M2-05 the Direct-mode MANTA (`config` with `shipId: 'manta'`, `powerUpMode: 'direct'`): `zone-a-manta` (the whole stage), `zone-a-manta-boss` (the boss, full loadout), `zone-a-manta-deaths` (the weaver, Arcade penalty), and since M2-06 two co-op games (`config.coop`): `zone-a-coop` (two 4-way bots, player 2 from tick 300), `zone-a-coop-deaths` (a weaving player 2 from tick 120 dying and continuing with START)): `{ name, description, stageId, config, godMode, bot: 'four-way' \| 'weaver' \| 'toggler' \| 'burster' (the last two M2-16), p2?: { bot, joinTick } }` — `p2` = player 2's pilot and the tick of its first START (then START every other tick while it may join). Since M3-02 one of the visual & mechanic extras: `zone-a-extras` — the MANTA over the whole of zone A with `slowdown`, `graze`, `deathBomb` 8 and `blackHole` all on, flown by the `bomber` bot (a `four-way` bot that presses `Special` for one tick every `BOMB_THROW_TICKS` 90 ticks), so the vortices' pull, their swallowed bullets, their lightning and the grazes are all in its hashes |
| `weaverBot()` | `golden.ts` | → a careless `PlaytestBot` (`weaver`): up / down 40 ticks each, never dodges — the death scenarios (`zone-a-deaths`, `zone-a-manta-deaths`, M2-11's `zone-b-deaths`, M2-13's `zone-f-deaths`, M2-14's `zone-h-deaths` and — with god mode, so it outlives the ARK's time limit — `zone-i-escape`) |
| `bomberBot()`, `BOMB_THROW_TICKS` | `golden.ts` | M3-02 → the 4-way bot that also presses `Special` for one tick every 90 ticks (`zone-a-extras`), so the Direct ship spends every bomb the stage's yellow items stock; `90` |
| `fireButtonBot(pattern)`, `TOGGLE_TAP_TICKS`, `BURST_CYCLE_TICKS` | `golden.ts` | M2-16 → the 4-way bot with a fire button: `'tap'` (`toggler`) presses `Shot` for one tick every `TOGGLE_TAP_TICKS` (150), `'burst'` (`burster`) holds `Shot` and `Sub` for the first half of every `BURST_CYCLE_TICKS` (90) — `golden-autofire.test.ts` checks both runs fire as their mode says and desync under `'always'` |
| `recordGolden(scenario)` | `golden.ts` | Records a scenario through `createReplayRecorder` + `createReplayGame` until `stageClear`, `gameOver` or `DEFAULT_MAX_TICKS` → `{ replay, outcome }` |
| `playGolden(replay, content?, observe?)` | `golden.ts` | Plays it back into a fresh session → `{ report: DesyncReport, outcome, world }` (`world` — M2-07 — the session's World after its last tick, for checks of what the run went through; `content` — M2-08 — default the shipped content, a variant to prove what the simulation ignores; `observe(world)` — M2-12 — called after every tick, e.g. to see where a death happened) |
| `readGolden(name)`, `writeGolden(scenario, replay, outcome)`, `formatGolden(…)`, `goldenPath(name)` | `golden.ts` | Read / write / format (`JSON.stringify`, 2-space indent, final newline) `test/golden/<name>.replay.json` |
| `GoldenFile`, `GoldenOutcome`, `GoldenPlayer2` | `golden.ts` | A `ReplayJson` + `description` + `expected: { status, ticks, score, lives, deathTicks, bossDefeated, p2? }`; `p2` (co-op runs, M2-06) = `{ score, lives, deathTicks, continues }` of player 2 |
| `GOLDEN_BUILD_ID`, `GOLDEN_UPDATE_ENV` | `golden.ts` | `'golden'`; `'SHMUP_GOLDEN_UPDATE'` |
| `DEMO_SCENARIOS`, `DemoScenario`, `DEMO_TICKS`, `EXAMPLE_DEMO`, `EXAMPLE_DEMO_TICKS` | `demos.ts` | M2-15: the attract loop's nine demos in content (path) order — `{ id, description, stageId, config (seed, ship), ticks? }`: the 4-way bot with god mode for `DEMO_TICKS` (2,400 — 40 s) from each zone's start, seeds 101–109, zones B, E and H in the MANTA; the format sample `content/demos/example.replay.json` (free flight, `EXAMPLE_DEMO_TICKS` 120) |
| `recordDemo(scenario)`, `formatDemo(scenario, replay)`, `writeDemo(scenario, replay)`, `readDemo(id)`, `demoPath(id)`, `DemoFile` | `demos.ts` | M2-15: records a demo on the shipped content (`createReplayRecorder` + `createReplayGame`, build id `DEMO_BUILD_ID`) → the `Replay` (throws when the content has issues); the file's text (content header, id, description, then `encodeReplay`'s fields — `JSON.stringify`, 2-space indent, final newline); write (re-bless) / read (→ `{ file, replay }`, throws for a missing or invalid file) `content/demos/<id>.replay.json`; a `ReplayJson` + the content header, `id` and `description`. `demos.test.ts` plays every file back through `createDemoPlayback` and `playReplay` with every hash; with `SHMUP_GOLDEN_UPDATE=1` it re-records them first |
